    ...(await Promise.all(likes.map(async (l) => ({ kind: "like" as const, at: l.createdAt, person: await person(l.user), stamp: await stampRef(l.photo) })))),
  ];
  return items.sort((a, b) => b.at.getTime() - a.at.getTime()).slice(0, ACTIVITY_MAX);
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/admin.ts
====================================================================================================

import { z } from "zod";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { badRequest, notFound } from "@/server/errors";
import { assertAdmin } from "@/server/permissions";

export const userFlagsSchema = z.object({ geofenceExempt: z.boolean() }).strict();

/**
 * Admin-only: mark an account geofence-exempt (App Review / QA). Exempt accounts can stamp from anywhere, but
 * everything they stamp is visible only to themselves.
 */
export async function setUserFlags(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string, raw: unknown) {
  assertAdmin(actor);
  const { geofenceExempt } = userFlagsSchema.parse(raw);
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: { id: true, deletedAt: true } });
  if (!user || user.deletedAt) throw notFound("User not found");
  return db.user.update({ where: { id: user.id }, data: { geofenceExempt }, select: { handle: true, geofenceExempt: true } });
}

/**
 * Bootstrap staff roles from the command line (`pnpm user:role <email> <USER|MODERATOR|ADMIN>`). Production has
 * no demo seed, so this is how the first admin and moderators are created: sign up normally, then promote.
 */
export const staffRoleSchema = z.enum(["USER", "MODERATOR", "ADMIN"]);

export async function setStaffRoleByEmail(db: Db, email: string, role: unknown) {
  const r = staffRoleSchema.parse(role);
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { email: email.trim().toLowerCase() } });
  if (!user || user.deletedAt) throw badRequest(`No active account with email ${email}`);
  return db.user.update({ where: { id: user.id }, data: { role: r }, select: { handle: true, role: true } });
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/auth.ts
====================================================================================================

import { Prisma, type SessionClient } from "@prisma/client";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { hashPassword, verifyPassword, getDummyHash } from "@/lib/auth/password";
import { createSession, destroySession } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { MINUTE_MS } from "@/lib/time";
import { isOldEnough, MIN_AGE, parseBirthdate } from "@/lib/age";
import { assertCleanText, conflict, ServiceError, tooMany } from "@/server/errors";

/** Bump when the Terms or Privacy Policy change materially; users who accepted an older version can be re-prompted. */
export const CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION = "2026-10-02";

export const signupSchema = z
  .object({
    email: z.string().trim().toLowerCase().email().max(200),
    password: z.string().min(10, "Password must be at least 10 characters").max(200),
    displayName: z.string().trim().min(1).max(60),
    handle: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase()
      .regex(/^[a-z0-9_]{3,24}$/, "Handle: 3-24 letters, numbers, underscore"),
    acceptTerms: z.literal(true, { errorMap: () => ({ message: "You need to accept the Terms and Privacy Policy" }) }),
    /** YYYY-MM-DD. Read once for the 21+ check and never stored. */
    birthdate: z.string().refine((v) => parseBirthdate(v) !== null, "Enter your birthdate as YYYY-MM-DD"),
  })
  .strict();

export const loginSchema = z.object({ email: z.string().trim().toLowerCase().email().max(200), password: z.string().max(200) }).strict();

export const UNDERAGE_MESSAGE = `You need to be ${MIN_AGE} or older to use Nostos`;

export async function signup(db: Db, input: unknown, opts: { client?: SessionClient; now?: Date } = {}) {
  const { birthdate, ...data } = signupSchema.parse(input);
  // The age gate comes before anything else touches the database. A refused attempt stores nothing about the person:
  // no account, no email, no birthdate, no log row. The birthdate goes out of scope here and is never written anywhere.
  if (!isOldEnough(birthdate, opts.now)) throw new ServiceError(403, "underage", UNDERAGE_MESSAGE);
  assertCleanText({ handle: data.handle, "display name": data.displayName });
  const existing = await db.user.findFirst({ where: { OR: [{ email: data.email }, { handle: data.handle }] } });
  if (existing) throw conflict(existing.email === data.email ? "Email already registered" : "Handle taken");
  // Public signup always creates a plain USER. Staff roles come from the command line or the seed.
  const passwordHash = await hashPassword(data.password);
  const user = await db.user
    .create({
      data: {
        email: data.email, handle: data.handle, displayName: data.displayName, passwordHash, role: "USER",
        termsAcceptedAt: new Date(), termsVersion: CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION,
      },
    })
    .catch((err: unknown) => {
      // Lost a race with a concurrent signup for the same email/handle.
      if (err instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError && err.code === "P2002") throw conflict("Email or handle already taken");
      throw err;
    });
  const session = await createSession(db, user.id, { client: opts.client });
  return { user, session };
}

// Login throttle, stored in Postgres so it works across serverless instances: at most 10 failed attempts per
// (email, client IP) per 15 minutes. Keying on the IP too means a stranger can't lock someone out from
// elsewhere (their failures only throttle their own IP). A per-IP ceiling across all emails (50 / 15 min)
// bounds how fast one client can grow the table or spray many accounts. The check, password verification
// and failure insert run under a transaction-scoped advisory lock on (email, IP), so parallel requests can't
// all slip under the limit. Old rows are pruned by the cron job.
export const LOGIN_FAIL_WINDOW_MS = 15 * MINUTE_MS;
export const LOGIN_MAX_FAILS = 10;
export const LOGIN_MAX_FAILS_PER_IP = 50;

export async function login(
  db: Db,
  input: z.infer<typeof loginSchema>,
  meta: { ip: string; client?: SessionClient } = { ip: "unknown" },
  now = new Date(),
) {
  const { email, password } = loginSchema.parse(input);
  const ip = meta.ip.slice(0, 64) || "unknown";
  const since = new Date(now.getTime() - LOGIN_FAIL_WINDOW_MS);
  const user = await db.$transaction(
    async (tx) => {
      await tx.$executeRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${`login:${email}:${ip}`}))`;
      const [pair, perIp] = await Promise.all([
        tx.loginAttempt.count({ where: { email, ip, createdAt: { gt: since } } }),
        tx.loginAttempt.count({ where: { ip, createdAt: { gt: since } } }),
      ]);
      if (pair >= LOGIN_MAX_FAILS || perIp >= LOGIN_MAX_FAILS_PER_IP) throw tooMany("Too many failed attempts. Try again in 15 minutes.");
      const found = await tx.user.findUnique({ where: { email } });
      const ok = await verifyPassword(password, found?.passwordHash ?? (await getDummyHash()));
      if (!found || !ok || found.deletedAt) {
        await tx.loginAttempt.create({ data: { email, ip, createdAt: now } });
        return null;
      }
      if (found.suspendedAt) throw new ServiceError(403, "account_suspended", "This account has been suspended for breaking our rules.");
      await tx.loginAttempt.deleteMany({ where: { email, ip } });
      return found;
    },
    { timeout: 15_000, maxWait: 10_000 },
  );
  if (!user) throw new ServiceError(401, "invalid_credentials", "Wrong email or password");
  const session = await createSession(db, user.id, { client: meta.client, now });
  return { user, session };
}

export async function pruneLoginAttempts(db: Db, now = new Date()) {
  const { count } = await db.loginAttempt.deleteMany({ where: { createdAt: { lt: new Date(now.getTime() - 24 * 3600_000) } } });
  return count;
}

export async function logout(db: Db, token: string | undefined) {
  await destroySession(db, token);
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/blocks.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Blocking (App Store Guideline 1.2). If A blocks B:
 *  - follows and pending follow requests between A and B are removed in both directions and can't be re-created
 *    by either side;
 *  - neither sees the other's stamps or profile, and they don't find each other in search, lists, feed, activity or likes.
 */
import type { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { badRequest, notFound } from "@/server/errors";
import { requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";

/** Ids of everyone the user has blocked or been blocked by. */
export async function blockedIdsFor(db: Db, userId: string | null | undefined): Promise<string[]> {
  if (!userId) return [];
  const rows = await db.block.findMany({
    where: { OR: [{ blockerId: userId }, { blockedId: userId }] },
    select: { blockerId: true, blockedId: true },
  });
  return [...new Set(rows.map((r) => (r.blockerId === userId ? r.blockedId : r.blockerId)))];
}

export async function isBlockedEitherWay(db: Db, a: string, b: string) {
  return (await db.block.count({ where: { OR: [{ blockerId: a, blockedId: b }, { blockerId: b, blockedId: a }] } })) > 0;
}

async function target(db: Db, handle: string) {
  // Suspended accounts can still be blocked (so a later unsuspension doesn't surprise anyone).
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: { id: true, handle: true, deletedAt: true } });
  if (!user || user.deletedAt) throw notFound("User not found");
  return user;
}

/**
 * Takes the follow locks for both directions of a pair (fixed order, so two blocks can't deadlock). follow(),
 * unfollow(), decideFollowRequest() and approveAllFollowRequests() take the same locks, so a follow or an approval
 * can't slip in between a block's check and its commit.
 */
export async function lockPair(tx: Prisma.TransactionClient, a: string, b: string) {
  for (const key of [`follow:${a}:${b}`, `follow:${b}:${a}`].sort()) await tx.$executeRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${key}))`;
}

async function block(db: Db, blockerId: string, blockedId: string, now: Date) {
  if (blockerId === blockedId) throw badRequest("You can't block yourself");
  await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    await lockPair(tx, blockerId, blockedId);
    await tx.block.upsert({
      where: { blockerId_blockedId: { blockerId, blockedId } },
      create: { blockerId, blockedId, createdAt: now },
      update: {},
    });
    await tx.follow.deleteMany({ where: { OR: [{ followerId: blockerId, followeeId: blockedId }, { followerId: blockedId, followeeId: blockerId }] } });
    await tx.followRequest.deleteMany({ where: { OR: [{ requesterId: blockerId, targetId: blockedId }, { requesterId: blockedId, targetId: blockerId }] } });
  });
  return { blocked: true };
}

export async function blockUser(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const other = await target(db, handle);
  return block(db, me.userId, other.id, now);
}

export async function unblockUser(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const other = await target(db, handle);
  await db.block.deleteMany({ where: { blockerId: me.userId, blockedId: other.id } });
  return { blocked: false };
}

/** Your blocks, newest first. Handles only: a blocked pair always gets the limited view. */
export async function listBlocks(db: Db, actor: Actor | null) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const rows = await db.block.findMany({
    where: { blockerId: me.userId, blocked: { deletedAt: null } },
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    select: { createdAt: true, blocked: { select: { handle: true } } },
  });
  return rows.map((r) => ({ handle: r.blocked.handle, blockedAt: r.createdAt }));
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/geofence.ts
====================================================================================================

import type { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";
import { distanceMeters, GEOFENCE_METERS, isValidLatLng, withinGeofence } from "@/lib/geo";
import { badRequest, forbidden, tooMany } from "@/server/errors";

/** One stamp per person per place per 15 minutes. */
export const STAMP_COOLDOWN_MS = 15 * 60_000;

/** Server-side geofence check from submitted coordinates (rule 1). The radius is the place's own. */
export function assertInsideGeofence(pos: { lat: number; lng: number }, place: { lat: number; lng: number; geofenceMeters?: number }) {
  if (!isValidLatLng(pos)) throw badRequest("Invalid coordinates");
  const radius = place.geofenceMeters ?? GEOFENCE_METERS;
  if (!withinGeofence(pos, place, radius)) {
    const d = Math.round(distanceMeters(pos, place));
    throw forbidden(`You need to be within ${radius} m of this place to stamp it (you're ${d} m away)`);
  }
}

/**
 * Serializes concurrent stamps for the same (user, place) so the cooldown can't be raced. Must run inside a transaction.
 */
export async function lockAndCheckCooldown(tx: Prisma.TransactionClient, key: { userId: string; venueId: string }, now: Date) {
  await tx.$executeRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${`${key.userId}:${key.venueId}:STAMP`}))`;
  const since = new Date(now.getTime() - STAMP_COOLDOWN_MS);
  const last = await tx.photo.findFirst({ where: { userId: key.userId, venueId: key.venueId, createdAt: { gt: since } }, orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" } });
  if (last) {
    const waitMin = Math.ceil((last.createdAt.getTime() + STAMP_COOLDOWN_MS - now.getTime()) / 60_000);
    throw tooMany(`You already stamped this place. Try again in ${waitMin} min.`);
  }
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/image.ts
====================================================================================================

import sharp from "sharp";
import { MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES, sniffImage, type ImageKind } from "@/lib/storage";
import { badRequest, ServiceError } from "@/server/errors";

export const MAX_IMAGE_DIMENSION = 2048;

/**
 * Validates and re-encodes an upload. Size limit and magic-byte type check first (client Content-Type is
 * ignored), then a full decode + re-encode with sharp. It applies EXIF orientation, caps each side at 2048px
 * and writes a fresh file with **no metadata** (EXIF/GPS/XMP/ICC dropped), so a phone's location never
 * leaks through a photo (rule 5). Anything sharp can't decode is rejected as unsupported.
 */
export async function processImage(input: Buffer): Promise<{ bytes: Buffer; kind: ImageKind }> {
  if (input.length === 0) throw badRequest("Empty file");
  if (input.length > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES) throw new ServiceError(413, "too_large", "Photos must be 5 MB or smaller");
  const kind = sniffImage(input);
  if (!kind) throw new ServiceError(415, "unsupported_type", "Only JPEG, PNG or WebP images are allowed");
  try {
    const pipeline = sharp(input, { failOn: "error", limitInputPixels: 50_000_000 })
      .rotate()
      .resize({ width: MAX_IMAGE_DIMENSION, height: MAX_IMAGE_DIMENSION, fit: "inside", withoutEnlargement: true });
    const out =
      kind.ext === "png" ? pipeline.png({ compressionLevel: 9 }) : kind.ext === "webp" ? pipeline.webp({ quality: 85 }) : pipeline.jpeg({ quality: 85 });
    return { bytes: await out.toBuffer(), kind };
  } catch {
    throw new ServiceError(415, "unsupported_type", "That image couldn't be read. Try a JPEG, PNG or WebP photo.");
  }
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/likes.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Likes on stamps: one per person per stamp, not your own, not on hidden or removed stamps, only on stamps you can see.
 * Counts exclude App Review likes. Likes never affect anything else (rule 10): they don't rank, feature or unlock.
 */
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { badRequest, notFound } from "@/server/errors";
import { requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { subjectSelect } from "@/server/privacy";
import { consumeRate } from "@/server/rate-limit";
import { assertVisible } from "@/server/services/stamps";

export type LikeSummary = { count: number; viewerLiked: boolean };

/** Like counts (App Review likes excluded) and whether the viewer liked each stamp. */
export async function likeSummaries(db: Db, stampIds: readonly string[], viewerId: string | null | undefined): Promise<Map<string, LikeSummary>> {
  const ids = [...new Set(stampIds)];
  const out = new Map<string, LikeSummary>(ids.map((id) => [id, { count: 0, viewerLiked: false }]));
  if (!ids.length) return out;
  const [counts, mine] = await Promise.all([
    // Suspended accounts' likes don't show in counts (deleted accounts' likes are gone).
    db.photoLike.groupBy({ by: ["photoId"], where: { photoId: { in: ids }, countable: true, user: { suspendedAt: null } }, _count: { _all: true } }),
    viewerId ? db.photoLike.findMany({ where: { photoId: { in: ids }, userId: viewerId }, select: { photoId: true } }) : Promise.resolve([]),
  ]);
  for (const c of counts) out.get(c.photoId)!.count = c._count._all;
  for (const m of mine) out.get(m.photoId)!.viewerLiked = true;
  return out;
}

/** A stamp the viewer can see (hidden and removed ones 404 even for staff: nobody likes those), or 404. */
async function likeableStamp(db: Db, actor: Actor, stampId: string) {
  const row = await db.photo.findUnique({
    where: { id: stampId },
    select: { id: true, userId: true, hiddenAt: true, removedAt: true, countable: true, user: { select: subjectSelect } },
  });
  if (!row || row.hiddenAt || row.removedAt) throw notFound("Stamp not found");
  await assertVisible(db, actor, row);
  return row;
}

export async function likeStamp(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, stampId: string, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const stamp = await likeableStamp(db, me, stampId);
  if (stamp.userId === me.userId) throw badRequest("You can't like your own stamp");
  await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    // Per (user, stamp) lock: a double tap can't hit the unique key and 500.
    await tx.$executeRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${`like:${me.userId}:${stampId}`}))`;
    const existing = await tx.photoLike.findUnique({ where: { userId_photoId: { userId: me.userId, photoId: stampId } } });
    if (existing) return;
    await consumeRate(tx, me.userId, "like", now);
    await tx.photoLike.create({ data: { userId: me.userId, photoId: stampId, createdAt: now, countable: !me.geofenceExempt } });
  });
  return { liked: true, ...(await likeSummaries(db, [stampId], me.userId)).get(stampId)! };
}

export async function unlikeStamp(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, stampId: string) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  // Unliking works even if the stamp has since become invisible to you: it only removes your own row.
  await db.photoLike.deleteMany({ where: { userId: me.userId, photoId: stampId } });
  // The count is only reported for a stamp you can still see.
  const visible = await likeableStamp(db, me, stampId).then(() => true, () => false);
  return { liked: false, ...(visible ? (await likeSummaries(db, [stampId], me.userId)).get(stampId)! : { count: 0, viewerLiked: false }) };
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/me.ts
====================================================================================================

import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { badRequest, notFound } from "@/server/errors";
import { requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { avatarUrl } from "@/server/services/people";

/** The signed-in account as the app needs it at launch: identity, role and settings. */
export async function getMe(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: me.userId },
    select: {
      id: true, email: true, handle: true, displayName: true, role: true, createdAt: true, reputation: true,
      termsAcceptedAt: true, termsVersion: true, geofenceExempt: true, isPrivate: true, bio: true, avatarKey: true, verifiedAt: true,
    },
  });
  if (!user) throw notFound("Account not found");
  const pendingFollowRequests = await db.followRequest.count({ where: { targetId: me.userId, requester: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } } });
  return {
    user: {
      id: user.id, email: user.email, handle: user.handle, displayName: user.displayName, role: user.role,
      memberSince: user.createdAt,
      /** Private: returned only to the account itself (and moderators on profiles). */
      reputation: user.reputation,
      isPrivate: user.isPrivate,
      bio: user.bio,
      avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(user.avatarKey, now),
      verified: user.verifiedAt !== null,
    },
    social: { pendingFollowRequests },
    /** Read-only account settings the app displays. None are user-editable yet. */
    settings: {
      /** App Review / QA account: can stamp anywhere, but its stamps are visible only to itself. Set by an admin only. */
      geofenceExempt: user.geofenceExempt,
    },
    terms: {
      acceptedVersion: user.termsVersion,
      acceptedAt: user.termsAcceptedAt,
      currentVersion: CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION,
      /** True when the app should ask the user to accept the current Terms and Privacy Policy. */
      needsAcceptance: user.termsVersion !== CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION,
    },
  };
}

/** Records acceptance of the current Terms/Privacy (when /api/me says needsAcceptance). */
export async function acceptCurrentTerms(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const body = (raw ?? {}) as { version?: unknown };
  if (body.version !== CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION) throw badRequest(`Accept the current terms version (${CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION})`);
  await db.user.update({ where: { id: me.userId }, data: { termsVersion: CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION, termsAcceptedAt: now } });
  return { acceptedVersion: CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION, acceptedAt: now };
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/moderation.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Flags and moderation.
 *
 * - Anyone signed in can flag a stamp they can see, or an account. Flagging never hides anything by itself; it queues
 *   the item for moderators and writes a FLAGGED event.
 * - Exception (spec): 3 open flags on a stamp from 3 distinct *eligible* users auto-hide it pending review
 *   (AUTO_HIDE event, actor = system). Eligible = role USER, account at least 7 days old, reputation >= 0.5.
 *   Staff, brand-new sock puppets and low-reputation accounts can still flag (it goes to the queue) but never count.
 * - One OPEN flag per user per item; once a moderator reviews it, the same user may flag it again.
 * - Only MODERATOR/ADMIN can HIDE, DISMISS (flags unfounded; undoes an *auto*-hide only) or RESTORE.
 * - Accounts can be reported too (targetType USER, e.g. from a profile). Moderators can SUSPEND an abusive account
 *   (can't sign in, all sessions revoked; its content stays until moderated) or UNSUSPEND it (Guideline 1.2).
 * - Stamps of deleted accounts stay hidden for good: RESTORE is refused and DISMISS never un-hides them.
 * - Nothing is ever deleted: hiding sets hidden_at. Every action writes an append-only moderation_event in the
 *   same transaction (rule 4; the table rejects UPDATE/DELETE at the database level).
 *
 * The API says STAMP; the database enum keeps its old name, PHOTO (a stamp is a row in the photo table).
 */
import type { ContentType, Prisma } from "@prisma/client";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { badRequest, conflict, notFound, tooMany } from "@/server/errors";
import { assertCanModerateContent, assertModerator, requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { uploadUrl } from "@/server/media";
import { subjectSelect } from "@/server/privacy";
import { assertVisible } from "@/server/services/stamps";

export const AUTO_HIDE_THRESHOLD = 3;
export const AUTO_HIDE_MIN_ACCOUNT_AGE_MS = 7 * 24 * 3600_000;
export const AUTO_HIDE_MIN_REPUTATION = 0.5;
const FLAGS_PER_HOUR = 20;

export type ApiTargetType = "STAMP" | "USER";
const toDb = (t: ApiTargetType): ContentType => (t === "STAMP" ? "PHOTO" : "USER");
const toApi = (t: ContentType): ApiTargetType => (t === "USER" ? "USER" : "STAMP");

/** Moderators read these first: a minor on the app and someone being followed around are the worst reports. */
export const URGENT_CATEGORIES = ["UNDERAGE", "STALKING"] as const;
export const flagCategorySchema = z.enum(["SPAM", "HARASSMENT", "SEXUAL_OR_VIOLENT", "NOT_AT_PLACE", "STALKING", "UNDERAGE", "PRIVATE_INFO", "OTHER"]);

export const flagSchema = z
  .object({
    targetType: z.enum(["STAMP", "USER"]),
    /** Content id; for USER targets, the account's id or handle. */
    targetId: z.string().min(1).max(64),
    reason: z.string().trim().min(3, "Say briefly what's wrong").max(300),
    /** Defaults to OTHER. */
    category: flagCategorySchema.default("OTHER"),
  })
  .strict();

const CONTENT_ACTIONS = ["HIDE", "DISMISS", "RESTORE"] as const;
const USER_ACTIONS = ["SUSPEND", "UNSUSPEND", "DISMISS"] as const;

export const moderateSchema = z
  .object({
    targetType: z.enum(["STAMP", "USER"]),
    targetId: z.string().min(1).max(64),
    action: z.enum(["HIDE", "DISMISS", "RESTORE", "SUSPEND", "UNSUSPEND"]),
    note: z.string().trim().max(300).optional(),
  })
  .strict()
  .refine(
    (m) => (m.targetType === "USER" ? (USER_ACTIONS as readonly string[]) : (CONTENT_ACTIONS as readonly string[])).includes(m.action),
    { message: "Accounts can be suspended, unsuspended or dismissed; content can be hidden, dismissed or restored", path: ["action"] },
  );

/** `hiddenAt` is the suspension time for USER targets. `authorDeleted` is true when the author deleted their account. */
type Target = { id: string; userId: string; venueId: string | null; hiddenAt: Date | null; authorDeleted: boolean };

async function loadTarget(db: Prisma.TransactionClient | Db, type: ContentType, idOrHandle: string): Promise<Target | null> {
  if (type === "USER") {
    const u = await db.user.findFirst({
      where: { OR: [{ id: idOrHandle }, { handle: idOrHandle.toLowerCase() }], deletedAt: null },
      select: { id: true, suspendedAt: true },
    });
    return u && { id: u.id, userId: u.id, venueId: null, hiddenAt: u.suspendedAt, authorDeleted: false };
  }
  const row = await db.photo.findUnique({
    where: { id: idOrHandle },
    select: { id: true, userId: true, venueId: true, hiddenAt: true, user: { select: { deletedAt: true } } },
  });
  return row && { id: row.id, userId: row.userId, venueId: row.venueId, hiddenAt: row.hiddenAt, authorDeleted: Boolean(row.user.deletedAt) };
}

async function setHidden(tx: Prisma.TransactionClient, type: ContentType, id: string, hiddenAt: Date | null) {
  if (type === "PHOTO") await tx.photo.update({ where: { id }, data: { hiddenAt } });
  else {
    await tx.user.update({ where: { id }, data: { suspendedAt: hiddenAt } });
    if (hiddenAt) await tx.session.deleteMany({ where: { userId: id } });
  }
}

async function lockTarget(tx: Prisma.TransactionClient, type: ContentType, id: string) {
  await tx.$executeRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${`moderation:${type}:${id}`}))`;
}

export async function flagContent(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const parsed = flagSchema.parse(raw);
  const input = { ...parsed, targetType: toDb(parsed.targetType) };
  const pre = await loadTarget(db, input.targetType, input.targetId);
  if (!pre || pre.hiddenAt) throw notFound(input.targetType === "USER" ? "User not found" : "Content not found");
  if (pre.userId === me.userId) throw badRequest(input.targetType === "USER" ? "You can't report yourself" : "You can't flag your own content");
  const targetId = pre.id; // USER targets may be given by handle; always store the id
  // You can only report a stamp you can see: a stranger can't probe a private account's stamps through the flag endpoint.
  if (input.targetType === "PHOTO") {
    const row = await db.photo.findUnique({ where: { id: pre.id }, select: { userId: true, hiddenAt: true, removedAt: true, countable: true, user: { select: subjectSelect } } });
    if (!row) throw notFound("Content not found");
    await assertVisible(db, me, row);
  }

  return db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    // Per-user lock makes the hourly limit exact under parallel requests; per-target lock orders flags on one item.
    await tx.$executeRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${`flagger:${me.userId}`}))`;
    const recent = await tx.flag.count({ where: { flaggerId: me.userId, createdAt: { gt: new Date(now.getTime() - 3600_000) } } });
    if (recent >= FLAGS_PER_HOUR) throw tooMany("You've flagged a lot recently. Try again later.");
    await lockTarget(tx, input.targetType, targetId);
    // Re-check under the lock: it may have been hidden while we waited.
    const target = await loadTarget(tx, input.targetType, targetId);
    if (!target || target.hiddenAt) throw notFound("Content not found");
    const existing = await tx.flag.findFirst({
      where: { targetType: input.targetType, targetId: targetId, flaggerId: me.userId, status: "OPEN" },
    });
    if (existing) throw conflict("You already flagged this");
    const flag = await tx.flag.create({
      data: { targetType: input.targetType, targetId: targetId, venueId: target.venueId, flaggerId: me.userId, reason: input.reason, category: input.category, createdAt: now },
    });
    await tx.moderationEvent.create({
      data: { action: "FLAGGED", targetType: input.targetType, targetId: targetId, actorId: me.userId, note: input.reason, createdAt: now },
    });

    let autoHidden = false;
    if (input.targetType === "PHOTO") {
      // Only eligible regular users count (see header): no venue accounts, staff, new or low-reputation accounts.
      const independent = await tx.flag.findMany({
        where: {
          targetType: "PHOTO",
          targetId: target.id,
          status: "OPEN",
          flagger: {
            role: "USER",
            geofenceExempt: false, // review/QA accounts never help auto-hide real users' photos
            createdAt: { lte: new Date(now.getTime() - AUTO_HIDE_MIN_ACCOUNT_AGE_MS) },
            reputation: { gte: AUTO_HIDE_MIN_REPUTATION },
          },
        },
        distinct: ["flaggerId"],
        select: { flaggerId: true },
      });
      if (independent.length >= AUTO_HIDE_THRESHOLD) {
        await setHidden(tx, "PHOTO", target.id, now);
        await tx.moderationEvent.create({
          data: { action: "AUTO_HIDE", targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: target.id, actorId: null, note: `${independent.length} distinct user flags`, createdAt: now },
        });
        autoHidden = true;
      }
    }
    return { flag, autoHidden };
  });
}

/** Moderator decision. Everyone else gets 403. */
export async function moderate(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const mod = assertCanModerateContent(actor);
  const parsed = moderateSchema.parse(raw);
  const input = { ...parsed, targetType: toDb(parsed.targetType) };
  const pre = await loadTarget(db, input.targetType, input.targetId);
  if (!pre) throw notFound(input.targetType === "USER" ? "User not found" : "Content not found");
  const targetId = pre.id;
  return db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    await lockTarget(tx, input.targetType, targetId);
    const target = await loadTarget(tx, input.targetType, targetId);
    if (!target) throw notFound("Content not found");
    const log = (action: "HIDE" | "DISMISS" | "RESTORE" | "SUSPEND" | "UNSUSPEND") =>
      tx.moderationEvent.create({ data: { action, targetType: input.targetType, targetId, actorId: mod.userId, note: input.note ?? null, createdAt: now } });
    const resolveFlags = () =>
      tx.flag.updateMany({ where: { targetType: input.targetType, targetId, status: "OPEN" }, data: { status: "RESOLVED" } });

    if (input.targetType === "USER") {
      if (target.userId === mod.userId) throw badRequest("You can't moderate your own account");
      if (input.action === "SUSPEND") {
        if (!target.hiddenAt) await setHidden(tx, "USER", targetId, now);
        await resolveFlags();
        await log("SUSPEND");
      } else if (input.action === "UNSUSPEND") {
        if (!target.hiddenAt) throw badRequest("This account isn't suspended");
        await setHidden(tx, "USER", targetId, null);
        await log("UNSUSPEND");
      } else {
        await resolveFlags();
        await log("DISMISS");
      }
      return { ...(await loadTarget(tx, "USER", targetId)), targetType: "USER" as const };
    }

    if (input.action === "HIDE") {
      if (!target.hiddenAt) await setHidden(tx, input.targetType, target.id, now);
      await resolveFlags();
      await log("HIDE");
    } else if (input.action === "DISMISS") {
      // Flags were unfounded: close them. Undo the hide only if the system auto-hid it; a moderator's own HIDE
      // stays until someone explicitly RESTOREs it.
      await resolveFlags();
      await log("DISMISS");
      if (target.hiddenAt && !target.authorDeleted) {
        const lastHide = await tx.moderationEvent.findFirst({
          where: { targetType: input.targetType, targetId: target.id, action: { in: ["AUTO_HIDE", "HIDE", "RESTORE"] } },
          orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
        });
        if (lastHide?.action === "AUTO_HIDE") {
          await setHidden(tx, input.targetType, target.id, null);
          await log("RESTORE");
        }
      }
    } else {
      if (!target.hiddenAt) throw badRequest("This content isn't hidden");
      if (target.authorDeleted && input.targetType === "PHOTO") {
        throw conflict("The author deleted their account, so their stamps stay hidden");
      }
      await setHidden(tx, input.targetType, target.id, null);
      await resolveFlags();
      await log("RESTORE");
    }
    return { ...(await loadTarget(tx, input.targetType, targetId)), targetType: toApi(input.targetType) };
  });
}

export type QueueItem = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof moderationQueue>>[number];

/** Open flags grouped by target, oldest first, with enough context to decide. Moderators only. */
export async function moderationQueue(db: Db, actor: Actor | null) {
  assertModerator(actor);
  const flags = await db.flag.findMany({
    where: { status: "OPEN" },
    orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" },
    include: { flagger: { select: { handle: true, role: true } }, venue: { select: { name: true, slug: true } } },
  });
  const groups = new Map<string, typeof flags>();
  for (const f of flags) {
    const key = `${f.targetType}:${f.targetId}`;
    groups.set(key, [...(groups.get(key) ?? []), f]);
  }

  const ids = (t: ContentType) => [...groups.values()].filter((g) => g[0]!.targetType === t).map((g) => g[0]!.targetId);
  const user = { select: { handle: true, reputation: true, deletedAt: true } } as const;
  const authorOf = (u: { handle: string; deletedAt: Date | null }) => (u.deletedAt ? "Deleted user" : u.handle);
  const [photos, users, events] = await Promise.all([
    db.photo.findMany({ where: { id: { in: ids("PHOTO") } }, include: { user } }),
    db.user.findMany({ where: { id: { in: ids("USER") } }, select: { id: true, handle: true, displayName: true, reputation: true, suspendedAt: true, createdAt: true, deletedAt: true } }),
    db.moderationEvent.findMany({
      where: { targetId: { in: [...groups.values()].map((g) => g[0]!.targetId) } },
      orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" },
      include: { actor: { select: { handle: true } } },
    }),
  ]);
  const byId = new Map<string, { kind: ApiTargetType; preview: string; url?: string; author: string; authorRep: number; hiddenAt: Date | null; createdAt: Date }>();
  for (const p of photos) byId.set(p.id, { kind: "STAMP", preview: stampPreview(p), url: p.storageKey ? await uploadUrl(p.storageKey) : undefined, author: authorOf(p.user), authorRep: p.user.reputation, hiddenAt: p.hiddenAt, createdAt: p.createdAt });
  for (const u of users) {
    byId.set(u.id, { kind: "USER", preview: `Account: ${u.displayName}`, author: authorOf(u), authorRep: u.reputation, hiddenAt: u.suspendedAt, createdAt: u.createdAt });
  }

  return [...groups.values()]
    .map((g) => {
      const first = g[0]!;
      const content = byId.get(first.targetId);
      return {
        targetType: toApi(first.targetType),
        targetId: first.targetId,
        venue: first.venue,
        content: content ?? null,
        flags: g.map((f) => ({
          id: f.id,
          reason: f.reason,
          category: f.category,
          createdAt: f.createdAt,
          by: f.flagger.handle,
          byRole: f.flagger.role,
        })),
        history: events.filter((e) => e.targetId === first.targetId).map((e) => ({ action: e.action, by: e.actor?.handle ?? "system", note: e.note, at: e.createdAt })),
      };
    })
    // Urgent categories first (underage, stalking), then auto-hidden items (hidden pending review), then oldest flag first.
    .sort(
      (a, b) =>
        Number(isUrgent(b.flags)) - Number(isUrgent(a.flags)) ||
        Number(Boolean(b.content?.hiddenAt)) - Number(Boolean(a.content?.hiddenAt)) ||
        a.flags[0]!.createdAt.getTime() - b.flags[0]!.createdAt.getTime(),
    );
}

const isUrgent = (flags: readonly { category: string }[]) => flags.some((f) => (URGENT_CATEGORIES as readonly string[]).includes(f.category));

export async function recentModerationLog(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, take = 30) {
  assertModerator(actor);
  return db.moderationEvent.findMany({ orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take, include: { actor: { select: { handle: true } } } });
}

/**
 * Recently hidden stamps and suspended accounts, newest first, so moderators can review and RESTORE /
 * UNSUSPEND (hidden items leave the queue once their flags are resolved). Stamps of deleted accounts are
 * excluded: they can never be restored.
 */
export async function recentlyHidden(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, take = 50) {
  assertModerator(actor);
  const [photos, users] = await Promise.all([
    db.photo.findMany({
      where: { hiddenAt: { not: null }, user: { deletedAt: null } },
      orderBy: { hiddenAt: "desc" },
      take,
      include: { user: { select: { handle: true, deletedAt: true } }, venue: { select: { name: true, slug: true } } },
    }),
    db.user.findMany({ where: { suspendedAt: { not: null }, deletedAt: null }, orderBy: { suspendedAt: "desc" }, take, select: { id: true, handle: true, displayName: true, suspendedAt: true } }),
  ]);
  type Item = { targetType: ApiTargetType; targetId: string; venue: { name: string; slug: string } | null; preview: string; url?: string; author: string; hiddenAt: Date };
  const items: Item[] = [
    ...(await Promise.all(
      photos.map(async (p) => ({
        targetType: "STAMP" as const, targetId: p.id, venue: p.venue, preview: stampPreview(p),
        url: p.storageKey ? await uploadUrl(p.storageKey) : undefined, author: p.user.handle, hiddenAt: p.hiddenAt!,
      })),
    )),
    ...users.map((u) => ({ targetType: "USER" as const, targetId: u.id, venue: null, preview: `Account: ${u.displayName}`, author: u.handle, hiddenAt: u.suspendedAt! })),
  ];
  return items.sort((a, b) => b.hiddenAt.getTime() - a.hiddenAt.getTime()).slice(0, take);
}

/** What a moderator reads for a stamp: the caption, or a note when it has only a photo. */
const stampPreview = (p: { caption: string }) => p.caption || "(photo only)";


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/osm-import.ts
====================================================================================================

import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { assignSlugs, neighborhoodOf, parseOverpass, type OverpassElement } from "@/lib/osm";

export type ImportResult = { fetched: number; created: number; updated: number; unchanged: number };

/**
 * Idempotent upsert on osm_id, in one transaction (all or nothing).
 * - New places get the default geofence.
 * - Slugs are assigned once, from OSM data alone (see assignSlugs), and never change afterwards.
 * - On re-import, coordinates always follow OSM (they define the geofence) and the neighborhood is recomputed from
 *   them. Name and address follow OSM.
 * - Places that disappear from OSM are left alone (they may have stamps).
 */
export async function importOsmVenues(db: Db, elements: readonly OverpassElement[]): Promise<ImportResult> {
  const venues = parseOverpass(elements);
  return db.$transaction(
    async (tx) => {
      const existing = await tx.venue.findMany({
        select: { id: true, slug: true, osmId: true, name: true, address: true, neighborhood: true, lat: true, lng: true },
      });
      const byOsm = new Map(existing.filter((v) => v.osmId).map((v) => [v.osmId!, v]));
      const reserved = new Set(existing.filter((v) => !v.osmId).map((v) => v.slug));
      const slugs = assignSlugs(venues, reserved);
      const usedSlugs = new Set(existing.map((v) => v.slug));
      const result: ImportResult = { fetched: venues.length, created: 0, updated: 0, unchanged: 0 };

      for (const v of venues) {
        const current = byOsm.get(v.osmId);
        if (!current) {
          const slug = slugs.get(v.osmId)!;
          if (usedSlugs.has(slug)) throw new Error(`Slug collision for ${v.osmId}: "${slug}" is already used`);
          usedSlugs.add(slug);
          await tx.venue.create({
            data: { slug, osmId: v.osmId, name: v.name, address: v.address, neighborhood: v.neighborhood, lat: v.lat, lng: v.lng },
          });
          result.created++;
          continue;
        }
        const data: { lat?: number; lng?: number; neighborhood?: string; name?: string; address?: string } = {};
        if (current.lat !== v.lat || current.lng !== v.lng) {
          data.lat = v.lat;
          data.lng = v.lng;
        }
        const hood = neighborhoodOf(v.lat, v.lng) ?? current.neighborhood;
        if (hood !== current.neighborhood) data.neighborhood = hood;
        const namesDiffer = current.name !== v.name || current.address !== v.address;
        if (namesDiffer) Object.assign(data, { name: v.name, address: v.address });
        if (Object.keys(data).length) {
          await tx.venue.update({ where: { id: current.id }, data });
          result.updated++;
        } else result.unchanged++;
      }
      return result;
    },
    { timeout: 120_000, maxWait: 10_000 },
  );
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/people.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * People: profiles, follows and follow requests, followers and following lists, search, profile settings.
 *
 * Privacy (src/server/privacy.ts): accounts are private by default. Following a public account is instant;
 * following a private one creates a request its owner approves or declines. Only approved follows exist in the
 * `follow` table.
 * A viewer without full access to a private profile gets the handle, the verified badge and a Request button only.
 *
 * Blocking removes follows and pending requests in both directions and prevents new ones (blocks.ts).
 */
import sharp from "sharp";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { getStorage, MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES, newStorageKey, sniffImage, type BlobStorage } from "@/lib/storage";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { assertCleanText, badRequest, forbidden, notFound, ServiceError } from "@/server/errors";
import { isModerator, requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { followedAmong, personView, profileAccess, subjectSelect, toSubject, viewerOf, type PersonView, type SubjectRow } from "@/server/privacy";
import { consumeRate } from "@/server/rate-limit";
import { blockedIdsFor, isBlockedEitherWay, lockPair } from "@/server/services/blocks";
import { uploadUrl } from "@/server/media";

export type FollowState = "none" | "requested" | "following";

/** Signed URL for a profile photo, or null. */
export const avatarUrl = async (key: string | null, now = new Date()) => (key ? uploadUrl(key, now) : null);

async function activeUser(db: Db, handle: string, opts: { allowSuspended?: boolean } = {}) {
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: subjectSelect });
  if (!user || user.deletedAt || (user.suspendedAt && !opts.allowSuspended)) throw notFound("User not found");
  return user;
}

/** The viewer's follow state toward each of `ids` (approved follows and pending requests). */
async function followStates(db: Db, viewerId: string | null | undefined, ids: readonly string[]) {
  const out = new Map<string, FollowState>();
  if (!viewerId || !ids.length) return out;
  const [follows, requests] = await Promise.all([
    followedAmong(db, viewerId, ids),
    db.followRequest.findMany({ where: { requesterId: viewerId, targetId: { in: [...ids] } }, select: { targetId: true } }),
  ]);
  for (const r of requests) out.set(r.targetId, "requested");
  for (const id of follows) out.set(id, "following");
  return out;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Following and requests
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Follow a public account (instant) or request to follow a private one. Idempotent. */
export async function follow(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string, now = new Date()): Promise<{ state: FollowState }> {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const target = await activeUser(db, handle);
  if (target.id === me.userId) throw badRequest("You can't follow yourself");
  return db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    // Locks both directions of the pair (the same locks a block takes): a double tap can't create both a follow
    // and a request, and a block can't commit between the check below and the insert.
    await lockPair(tx, me.userId, target.id);
    if (await isBlockedEitherWay(tx as Db, me.userId, target.id)) throw forbidden("You can't follow this account");
    const [existing, pending, fresh] = await Promise.all([
      tx.follow.findUnique({ where: { followerId_followeeId: { followerId: me.userId, followeeId: target.id } } }),
      tx.followRequest.findUnique({ where: { requesterId_targetId: { requesterId: me.userId, targetId: target.id } } }),
      tx.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: target.id }, select: { isPrivate: true } }),
    ]);
    if (existing) return { state: "following" as const };
    if (pending && fresh.isPrivate) return { state: "requested" as const };
    await consumeRate(tx, me.userId, "follow", now);
    if (!fresh.isPrivate) {
      await tx.follow.create({ data: { followerId: me.userId, followeeId: target.id, createdAt: now } });
      await tx.followRequest.deleteMany({ where: { requesterId: me.userId, targetId: target.id } });
      return { state: "following" as const };
    }
    await tx.followRequest.create({ data: { requesterId: me.userId, targetId: target.id, createdAt: now } });
    return { state: "requested" as const };
  });
}

/** Unfollow, or cancel a pending request. Idempotent. */
export async function unfollow(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string): Promise<{ state: FollowState }> {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const target = await activeUser(db, handle, { allowSuspended: true });
  // Same pair lock as follow and approve: a cancel can't lose a race against its own approval.
  await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    await lockPair(tx, me.userId, target.id);
    await tx.follow.deleteMany({ where: { followerId: me.userId, followeeId: target.id } });
    await tx.followRequest.deleteMany({ where: { requesterId: me.userId, targetId: target.id } });
  });
  return { state: "none" };
}

/** Requests waiting for the signed-in user's decision, newest first. */
export async function listFollowRequests(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const rows = await db.followRequest.findMany({
    where: { targetId: me.userId, requester: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } },
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    take: 200,
    select: { createdAt: true, requester: { select: subjectSelect } },
  });
  const follows = await followedAmong(db, me.userId, rows.map((r) => r.requester.id));
  const viewer = viewerOf(me);
  return Promise.all(
    rows.map(async (r) => {
      const access = profileAccess(viewer, toSubject(r.requester), follows.has(r.requester.id));
      return {
        person: personView(access, { ...r.requester, verified: r.requester.verifiedAt !== null }, await avatarUrl(r.requester.avatarKey, now)),
        requestedAt: r.createdAt,
      };
    }),
  );
}

export const requestDecisionSchema = z.object({ approve: z.boolean() }).strict();

/** Approve or decline one request. Only requests addressed to the signed-in user can be found (404 otherwise). */
export async function decideFollowRequest(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const { approve } = requestDecisionSchema.parse(raw);
  const requester = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: { id: true, deletedAt: true, suspendedAt: true } });
  if (!requester || requester.deletedAt) throw notFound("No follow request from this account");
  return db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    await lockPair(tx, requester.id, me.userId);
    const { count } = await tx.followRequest.deleteMany({ where: { requesterId: requester.id, targetId: me.userId } });
    if (count === 0) throw notFound("No follow request from this account");
    if (!approve) return { approved: false };
    if (requester.suspendedAt || (await isBlockedEitherWay(tx as Db, me.userId, requester.id))) throw notFound("No follow request from this account");
    await tx.follow.upsert({
      where: { followerId_followeeId: { followerId: requester.id, followeeId: me.userId } },
      create: { followerId: requester.id, followeeId: me.userId, createdAt: now },
      update: {},
    });
    return { approved: true };
  });
}

/** Approve every pending request at once (offered after switching a private account to public). */
export async function approveAllFollowRequests(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  return db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    const pending = await tx.followRequest.findMany({ where: { targetId: me.userId }, select: { requesterId: true } });
    // Lock every pair first (the locks blocks and cancels take), then decide from what's still there.
    for (const r of [...pending].sort((a, b) => a.requesterId.localeCompare(b.requesterId))) await lockPair(tx, r.requesterId, me.userId);
    const blocked = await blockedIdsFor(tx as Db, me.userId);
    const rows = await tx.followRequest.findMany({
      where: { targetId: me.userId, requester: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null }, requesterId: { notIn: blocked } },
      select: { requesterId: true },
    });
    const ids = rows.map((r) => r.requesterId);
    if (!ids.length) return { approved: 0 };
    await tx.follow.createMany({ data: ids.map((id) => ({ followerId: id, followeeId: me.userId, createdAt: now })), skipDuplicates: true });
    await tx.followRequest.deleteMany({ where: { targetId: me.userId, requesterId: { in: ids } } });
    return { approved: ids.length };
  });
}

/** Remove someone who follows you. Idempotent. */
export async function removeFollower(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const other = await activeUser(db, handle, { allowSuspended: true });
  await db.follow.deleteMany({ where: { followerId: other.id, followeeId: me.userId } });
  return { removed: true as const };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Profiles and lists
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Stamps on a profile: not hidden or removed. App Review stamps count only for their author. */
export const profileStampsWhere = (userId: string, isSelf: boolean) =>
  ({ userId, hiddenAt: null, removedAt: null, ...(isSelf ? {} : { countable: true }) }) as const;

export async function getProfile(db: Db, handle: string, viewer: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({
    where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() },
    select: {
      ...subjectSelect, createdAt: true, reputation: true, bio: true,
    },
  });
  if (!user) throw notFound("User not found");
  const isSelf = viewer?.userId === user.id;
  // Deleted accounts can't be looked up, and blocked users (either direction) don't see each other.
  if (user.deletedAt || (user.suspendedAt && !isModerator(viewer))) throw notFound("User not found");
  if (viewer && !isSelf && (await isBlockedEitherWay(db, viewer.userId, user.id))) throw notFound("User not found");

  const [states, followsViewer] = await Promise.all([
    followStates(db, viewer?.userId, [user.id]),
    viewer && !isSelf
      ? db.follow.findUnique({ where: { followerId_followeeId: { followerId: user.id, followeeId: viewer.userId } } }).then(Boolean)
      : Promise.resolve(false),
  ]);
  const follow: FollowState = isSelf ? "none" : (states.get(user.id) ?? "none");
  const relationship = { isSelf, follow, followsYou: followsViewer, mutual: follow === "following" && followsViewer };
  const access = profileAccess(viewerOf(viewer), toSubject(user), follow === "following");
  const verified = user.verifiedAt !== null;

  if (access === "limited") {
    return { access: "limited" as const, user: { handle: user.handle, verified, isPrivate: true as const }, relationship };
  }

  const [followers, following, stamps, places] = await Promise.all([
    // Same people the lists show (active accounts).
    db.follow.count({ where: { followeeId: user.id, follower: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } } }),
    db.follow.count({ where: { followerId: user.id, followee: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } } }),
    db.photo.count({ where: profileStampsWhere(user.id, isSelf) }),
    db.photo.findMany({ where: profileStampsWhere(user.id, isSelf), distinct: ["venueId"], select: { venueId: true } }).then((r) => r.length),
  ]);
  return {
    access: "full" as const,
    user: {
      handle: user.handle,
      displayName: user.displayName,
      avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(user.avatarKey, now),
      bio: user.bio,
      verified,
      isPrivate: user.isPrivate,
      memberSince: user.createdAt,
      // Reputation is private: shown to the account itself and to moderators only (rule 7).
      reputation: isSelf || isModerator(viewer) ? user.reputation : null,
      counts: { stamps, places, followers, following },
    },
    relationship,
  };
}

export type ProfileView = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getProfile>>;

/** A list of people as the viewer may see them, with the viewer's follow state toward each. */
async function peopleView(db: Db, viewer: Actor | null, rows: SubjectRow[], now: Date) {
  const states = await followStates(db, viewer?.userId, rows.map((r) => r.id));
  const v = viewerOf(viewer);
  return Promise.all(
    rows.map(async (r) => {
      const state = states.get(r.id) ?? "none";
      const access = profileAccess(v, toSubject(r), state === "following");
      return {
        ...personView(access, { ...r, verified: r.verifiedAt !== null }, access === "full" ? await avatarUrl(r.avatarKey, now) : null),
        follow: state,
        isSelf: viewer?.userId === r.id,
      };
    }),
  );
}

/** Followers or following of a profile. Needs full access to the profile (403 `private_account` otherwise). */
export async function listConnections(db: Db, viewer: Actor | null, handle: string, which: "followers" | "following", now = new Date()) {
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: subjectSelect });
  if (!user || user.deletedAt || (user.suspendedAt && !isModerator(viewer))) throw notFound("User not found");
  const blocked = await blockedIdsFor(db, viewer?.userId);
  if (blocked.includes(user.id)) throw notFound("User not found");
  const follows = await followedAmong(db, viewer?.userId, [user.id]);
  if (profileAccess(viewerOf(viewer), toSubject(user), follows.has(user.id)) === "limited") {
    throw new ServiceError(403, "private_account", "This account is private. Follow it to see who they follow and who follows them.");
  }
  const active = { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null, id: { notIn: blocked } };
  const rows =
    which === "followers"
      ? (await db.follow.findMany({ where: { followeeId: user.id, follower: active }, orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: 500, select: { follower: { select: subjectSelect } } })).map((r) => r.follower)
      : (await db.follow.findMany({ where: { followerId: user.id, followee: active }, orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: 500, select: { followee: { select: subjectSelect } } })).map((r) => r.followee);
  return peopleView(db, viewer, rows, now);
}

/**
 * People search by handle, and by display name where the viewer may see it (public accounts, people they follow).
 * Matching a private account's display name would reveal it, so those match on the handle only.
 */
export async function searchUsers(db: Db, viewer: Actor | null, q: string, now = new Date()) {
  const query = q.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^@/, "");
  if (query.length < 2) return [];
  const nameVisible = [{ isPrivate: false }, ...(viewer ? [{ followers: { some: { followerId: viewer.userId } } }] : [])];
  const users = await db.user.findMany({
    where: {
      OR: [{ handle: { contains: query } }, { displayName: { contains: query, mode: "insensitive" }, OR: nameVisible }],
      deletedAt: null,
      suspendedAt: null,
      ...(viewer ? { id: { notIn: [viewer.userId, ...(await blockedIdsFor(db, viewer.userId))] } } : {}),
    },
    take: 20,
    orderBy: { handle: "asc" },
    select: subjectSelect,
  });
  return peopleView(db, viewer, users, now);
}

/** People you follow (approved follows only, so you have full access to each). */
export async function listFollowing(db: Db, viewer: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(viewer);
  const rows = await db.follow.findMany({
    where: { followerId: me.userId, followee: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } },
    orderBy: { followee: { handle: "asc" } },
    select: { followee: { select: { ...subjectSelect, following: { where: { followeeId: me.userId }, select: { followerId: true } } } } },
  });
  return Promise.all(
    rows.map(async ({ followee: f }) => ({
      handle: f.handle, displayName: f.displayName, avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(f.avatarKey, now), verified: f.verifiedAt !== null,
      mutual: f.following.length > 0,
    })),
  );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Profile settings
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const BIO_MAX = 150;

export const profileSettingsSchema = z
  .object({
    displayName: z.string().trim().min(1, "Enter a name").max(60, "Name: 60 characters max"),
    bio: z.string().trim().max(BIO_MAX, `Bio: ${BIO_MAX} characters max`),
    isPrivate: z.boolean(),
  })
  .partial()
  .strict();

async function settingsView(db: Db, userId: string, now: Date) {
  const [u, pendingRequests] = await Promise.all([
    db.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: userId }, select: { handle: true, displayName: true, bio: true, isPrivate: true, avatarKey: true, verifiedAt: true } }),
    db.followRequest.count({ where: { targetId: userId, requester: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } } }),
  ]);
  return {
    profile: { handle: u.handle, displayName: u.displayName, bio: u.bio, isPrivate: u.isPrivate, avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(u.avatarKey, now), verified: u.verifiedAt !== null },
    /** Still pending after switching to public: the app offers "Approve N pending requests?". */
    pendingRequests,
  };
}

/**
 * Edit display name, bio and the private toggle. Switching private to public doesn't approve pending requests;
 * that's a separate, explicit action (approveAllFollowRequests).
 */
export async function updateProfile(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const input = profileSettingsSchema.parse(raw);
  assertCleanText({ name: input.displayName, bio: input.bio });
  await db.user.update({ where: { id: me.userId }, data: input });
  return settingsView(db, me.userId, now);
}

export async function getProfileSettings(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  return settingsView(db, requireSignedIn(actor).userId, now);
}

export const AVATAR_SIZE = 512;

/** Profile photos: same checks as venue photos (size, magic bytes, full decode), then a square JPEG with no metadata. */
export async function processAvatar(input: Buffer) {
  if (input.length === 0) throw badRequest("Empty file");
  if (input.length > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES) throw new ServiceError(413, "too_large", "Photos must be 5 MB or smaller");
  if (!sniffImage(input)) throw new ServiceError(415, "unsupported_type", "Only JPEG, PNG or WebP images are allowed");
  try {
    return await sharp(input, { failOn: "error", limitInputPixels: 50_000_000 })
      .rotate()
      .resize({ width: AVATAR_SIZE, height: AVATAR_SIZE, fit: "cover" })
      .jpeg({ quality: 85 })
      .toBuffer();
  } catch {
    throw new ServiceError(415, "unsupported_type", "That image couldn't be read. Try a JPEG, PNG or WebP photo.");
  }
}

export async function setAvatar(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, bytes: Buffer, now = new Date(), storage: BlobStorage = getStorage()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const clean = await processAvatar(bytes);
  const before = await db.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: me.userId }, select: { avatarKey: true } });
  const key = newStorageKey("avatars", "jpg");
  await storage.put(key, clean, "image/jpeg");
  try {
    await db.user.update({ where: { id: me.userId }, data: { avatarKey: key } });
  } catch (err) {
    await storage.delete(key).catch(() => {});
    throw err;
  }
  if (before.avatarKey) await storage.delete(before.avatarKey).catch(() => {});
  return { avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(key, now) };
}

export async function removeAvatar(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, storage: BlobStorage = getStorage()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const before = await db.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: me.userId }, select: { avatarKey: true } });
  if (before.avatarKey) {
    await db.user.update({ where: { id: me.userId }, data: { avatarKey: null } });
    await storage.delete(before.avatarKey).catch(() => {});
  }
  return { avatarUrl: null };
}

export type PersonWithState = PersonView & { follow: FollowState; isSelf: boolean };


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/places.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Places: name, location and geofence. No live data of any kind (no crowd, no reports, no counts of who is there).
 */
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { distanceMeters } from "@/lib/geo";
import { notFound } from "@/server/errors";
import { OSM_ATTRIBUTION } from "@/lib/osm";

const placeSelect = { id: true, slug: true, name: true, address: true, neighborhood: true, lat: true, lng: true, geofenceMeters: true, osmId: true } as const;
type PlaceRow = { id: string; slug: string; name: string; address: string; neighborhood: string; lat: number; lng: number; geofenceMeters: number; osmId: string | null };

export type PlaceView = {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  address: string;
  neighborhood: string;
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  geofenceMeters: number;
  /** Imported from OpenStreetMap (show "© OpenStreetMap contributors"). */
  fromOsm: boolean;
};

const toView = ({ osmId, ...p }: PlaceRow): PlaceView => ({ ...p, fromOsm: osmId !== null });

/** ODbL attribution to show with a place list, when any place in it came from OpenStreetMap. */
export const attributionFor = (places: readonly { fromOsm: boolean }[]) => (places.some((p) => p.fromOsm) ? OSM_ATTRIBUTION : null);

export async function listPlaces(db: Db): Promise<PlaceView[]> {
  return (await db.venue.findMany({ orderBy: { name: "asc" }, select: placeSelect })).map(toView);
}

/**
 * Places near a position, nearest first, within `radiusM` (default 300 m: inside or close to the geofence). The server
 * recomputes the geofence again when a stamp is submitted; this list is only a convenience. The caller's own
 * position is used for the distance and never stored.
 */
export async function nearbyPlaces(db: Db, pos: { lat: number; lng: number }, radiusM = 300, limit = 20) {
  const all = await db.venue.findMany({ select: placeSelect });
  return all
    .map((p) => ({ ...toView(p), distanceM: Math.round(distanceMeters(pos, p)) }))
    .filter((p) => p.distanceM <= Math.max(radiusM, p.geofenceMeters))
    .sort((a, b) => a.distanceM - b.distanceM || a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
    .slice(0, limit)
    .map((p) => ({ ...p, inside: p.distanceM <= p.geofenceMeters }));
}

/** A place by id or slug. */
export async function getPlace(db: Db, key: string): Promise<PlaceView> {
  const p = await db.venue.findFirst({ where: { OR: [{ id: key }, { slug: key }] }, select: placeSelect });
  if (!p) throw notFound("Place not found");
  return toView(p);
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/songs.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Song search for the stamp composer. v1 stores metadata only (title, artist, art URL); there is no audio.
 * Proxied to Apple's public iTunes Search API through our backend so the phone never talks to Apple, with a short
 * timeout, a per-user rate limit, and no user identifiers sent upstream: only the search term, a fixed country and a
 * fixed limit. The terms-of-use question for this API is in OPEN-QUESTIONS.md (L1); nothing here decides it.
 */
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { containsObjectionable } from "@/lib/content-filter";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { badRequest, ServiceError } from "@/server/errors";
import { requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { consumeRate } from "@/server/rate-limit";

export const ITUNES_SEARCH_URL = "https://itunes.apple.com/search";
export const SONG_SEARCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 3000;
export const SONG_RESULTS = 15;

export type SongResult = { title: string; artist: string; album: string | null; artUrl: string | null; sourceId: string };

const itunesResponse = z.object({
  results: z.array(
    z.object({
      trackId: z.number(),
      trackName: z.string(),
      artistName: z.string(),
      collectionName: z.string().optional(),
      artworkUrl100: z.string().optional(),
    }).passthrough(),
  ),
});

/** Apple's art URLs end in `100x100bb.jpg`; ask for a larger rendition. Only https links on Apple's CDN are kept. */
function artFrom(url: string | undefined): string | null {
  if (!url) return null;
  try {
    const u = new URL(url);
    if (u.protocol !== "https:" || !/(^|\.)mzstatic\.com$/.test(u.hostname)) return null;
    return u.toString().replace(/\/\d+x\d+bb\./, "/300x300bb.");
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export const songQuerySchema = z.string().trim().min(2, "Type at least 2 characters").max(80, "Search is limited to 80 characters");

export async function searchSongs(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, rawQuery: unknown, now = new Date(), fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch): Promise<{ songs: SongResult[] }> {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const term = songQuerySchema.parse(rawQuery ?? "");
  await db.$transaction((tx) => consumeRate(tx, me.userId, "song_search", now));

  const url = new URL(ITUNES_SEARCH_URL);
  url.searchParams.set("term", term);
  url.searchParams.set("media", "music");
  url.searchParams.set("entity", "song");
  url.searchParams.set("country", "US");
  url.searchParams.set("limit", String(SONG_RESULTS));
  let body: unknown;
  try {
    // No cookies, no auth header, no user id: the request carries only the search term.
    const res = await fetchImpl(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(SONG_SEARCH_TIMEOUT_MS), headers: { accept: "application/json" }, credentials: "omit", redirect: "error" });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`upstream ${res.status}`);
    body = await res.json();
  } catch {
    throw new ServiceError(503, "song_search_unavailable", "Song search isn't available right now. Try again in a moment.");
  }
  const parsed = itunesResponse.safeParse(body);
  if (!parsed.success) throw badRequest("Song search returned something unexpected", "song_search_bad_response");
  const songs = parsed.data.results
    .filter((r) => r.trackName.length <= 200 && r.artistName.length <= 200 && !containsObjectionable(r.trackName) && !containsObjectionable(r.artistName))
    .map((r) => ({ title: r.trackName, artist: r.artistName, album: r.collectionName ?? null, artUrl: artFrom(r.artworkUrl100), sourceId: String(r.trackId) }));
  return { songs };
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/stamps.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Stamps: one visit to a place, with a photo, a caption, a song, or any mix (at least one). A stamp is a row in the
 * `photo` table (the name is kept to avoid migration churn); the API and the app say "stamp".
 *
 *  - Created only inside the place's geofence, checked here from the submitted coordinates (rule 1). Geofence-exempt
 *    (App Review) accounts may stamp from anywhere, and their stamps are visible only to themselves.
 *  - One stamp per person per place per 15 minutes, and a per-hour limit across places. EXIF is stripped (rule 5).
 *  - Visibility = the author's account privacy (src/server/privacy.ts#canSeeStamp). There is no per-stamp setting.
 *  - The author can edit the caption and the song (frozen while a moderator is looking at it) and "delete from
 *    profile", which sets removed_at for good. Nothing is ever deleted (rule 4).
 *  - Song data is metadata only; no audio. The art URL must come from Apple's artwork CDN.
 */
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { isValidLatLng } from "@/lib/geo";
import { getStorage, newStorageKey, type BlobStorage } from "@/lib/storage";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { uploadUrl } from "@/server/media";
import { assertCleanText, badRequest, conflict, notFound, ServiceError } from "@/server/errors";
import { assertCanEditOwnContent, isModerator, requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { authorResolver, canSeeStamp, subjectSelect, toSubject, type SubjectRow } from "@/server/privacy";
import { consumeRate } from "@/server/rate-limit";
import { assertInsideGeofence, lockAndCheckCooldown } from "@/server/services/geofence";
import { processImage } from "@/server/services/image";
import { isBlockedEitherWay } from "@/server/services/blocks";
import { likeSummaries, type LikeSummary } from "@/server/services/likes";
import { avatarUrl, profileStampsWhere } from "@/server/services/people";

export const CAPTION_MAX = 280;
export const STAMPS_PAGE = 30;
const SONG_FIELD_MAX = 200;

/** Album art only from Apple's artwork CDN, over https, so a stamp can't embed a tracking pixel from anywhere else. */
const artUrl = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .max(500)
  .refine((u) => {
    try {
      const url = new URL(u);
      return url.protocol === "https:" && /(^|\.)mzstatic\.com$/.test(url.hostname);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }, "Song art must be an https link from Apple's artwork servers");

/** Empty strings (multipart fields) mean "absent". */
const optional = <T extends z.ZodTypeAny>(schema: T) => z.preprocess((v) => (v === "" || v === null ? undefined : v), schema.optional());

const songShape = {
  songTitle: optional(z.string().trim().min(1).max(SONG_FIELD_MAX)),
  songArtist: optional(z.string().trim().min(1).max(SONG_FIELD_MAX)),
  songArtUrl: optional(artUrl),
  songSourceId: optional(z.string().trim().min(1).max(100)),
};

const checkSong = (m: { songTitle?: string; songArtist?: string; songArtUrl?: string; songSourceId?: string }, ctx: z.RefinementCtx) => {
  const any = m.songTitle !== undefined || m.songArtist !== undefined || m.songArtUrl !== undefined || m.songSourceId !== undefined;
  if (any && (m.songTitle === undefined || m.songArtist === undefined)) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: ["songTitle"], message: "A song needs a title and an artist" });
  }
};

export const stampMetaSchema = z
  .object({
    caption: z.string().trim().max(CAPTION_MAX, `Caption: ${CAPTION_MAX} characters max`).default(""),
    lat: z.coerce.number().finite(),
    lng: z.coerce.number().finite(),
    /** Optional, stored but not shown in v1. */
    rating: optional(z.coerce.number().int("Rating: a whole number from 1 to 10").min(1, "Rating: 1 to 10").max(10, "Rating: 1 to 10")),
    ...songShape,
  })
  .strict()
  .superRefine(checkSong);

export const stampEditSchema = z
  .object({
    caption: z.string().trim().max(CAPTION_MAX, `Caption: ${CAPTION_MAX} characters max`),
    /** null removes the song. */
    song: z
      .object({
        title: z.string().trim().min(1).max(SONG_FIELD_MAX),
        artist: z.string().trim().min(1).max(SONG_FIELD_MAX),
        artUrl: artUrl.nullish(),
        sourceId: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100).nullish(),
      })
      .strict()
      .nullable(),
  })
  .partial()
  .strict()
  .refine((v) => Object.keys(v).length > 0, "Nothing to change");

type StampRow = {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
  storageKey: string | null;
  caption: string;
  songTitle: string | null;
  songArtist: string | null;
  songArtUrl: string | null;
  songSourceId: string | null;
  createdAt: Date;
  editedAt: Date | null;
  hiddenAt: Date | null;
  removedAt: Date | null;
  countable: boolean;
  user: SubjectRow;
  venue: { id: string; slug: string; name: string; neighborhood: string };
};

const stampSelect = {
  id: true, userId: true, storageKey: true, caption: true, songTitle: true, songArtist: true, songArtUrl: true, songSourceId: true,
  createdAt: true, editedAt: true, hiddenAt: true, removedAt: true, countable: true,
  user: { select: subjectSelect },
  venue: { select: { id: true, slug: true, name: true, neighborhood: true } },
} as const;

export type StampView = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof toStampViews>>[number];

/** Builds the wire shape. Callers have already decided each stamp is visible to `actor`. Never includes coordinates, keys or user ids. */
async function toStampViews(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, rows: readonly StampRow[], now: Date) {
  const likes = await likeSummaries(db, rows.map((r) => r.id), actor?.userId);
  const none: LikeSummary = { count: 0, viewerLiked: false };
  return Promise.all(
    rows.map(async (r) => ({
      id: r.id,
      photoUrl: r.storageKey ? await uploadUrl(r.storageKey, now) : null,
      caption: r.caption,
      song: r.songTitle ? { title: r.songTitle, artist: r.songArtist!, artUrl: r.songArtUrl, sourceId: r.songSourceId } : null,
      createdAt: r.createdAt,
      editedAt: r.editedAt,
      place: { id: r.venue.id, slug: r.venue.slug, name: r.venue.name, neighborhood: r.venue.neighborhood },
      author: { handle: r.user.handle, displayName: r.user.displayName, avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(r.user.avatarKey, now), verified: r.user.verifiedAt !== null },
      mine: actor?.userId === r.userId,
      likes: likes.get(r.id) ?? none,
      /** Staff see hidden and removed stamps (moderation); everyone else never receives them. */
      hidden: r.hiddenAt !== null,
      removed: r.removedAt !== null,
    })),
  );
}

/** Creates a stamp. `bytes` is optional: a stamp may be a caption, a song, or both with no photo. */
export async function createStamp(
  db: Db,
  actor: Actor | null,
  venueId: string,
  input: { bytes?: Buffer | null; meta: unknown },
  now = new Date(),
  storage: BlobStorage = getStorage(),
) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const meta = stampMetaSchema.parse(input.meta);
  const hasPhoto = Boolean(input.bytes && input.bytes.length > 0);
  if (!hasPhoto && !meta.caption && meta.songTitle === undefined) throw badRequest("A stamp needs a photo, a caption or a song");
  assertCleanText({ caption: meta.caption, "song title": meta.songTitle, "song artist": meta.songArtist });
  const place = await db.venue.findUnique({ where: { id: venueId }, select: { id: true, lat: true, lng: true, geofenceMeters: true } });
  if (!place) throw notFound("Place not found");
  if (me.geofenceExempt) {
    if (!isValidLatLng(meta)) throw badRequest("Invalid coordinates");
  } else assertInsideGeofence(meta, place);
  const image = hasPhoto ? await processImage(input.bytes!) : null;

  const key = image ? newStorageKey("photos", image.kind.ext) : null;
  let wrote = false;
  try {
    const row = await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await lockAndCheckCooldown(tx, { userId: me.userId, venueId }, now);
      await consumeRate(tx, me.userId, "post", now);
      const created = await tx.photo.create({
        data: {
          userId: me.userId, venueId, storageKey: key, mimeType: image?.kind.mime ?? null, caption: meta.caption, lat: meta.lat, lng: meta.lng,
          createdAt: now, countable: !me.geofenceExempt, rating: meta.rating ?? null,
          songTitle: meta.songTitle ?? null, songArtist: meta.songArtist ?? null, songArtUrl: meta.songArtUrl ?? null, songSourceId: meta.songSourceId ?? null,
        },
        select: stampSelect,
      });
      // Written last inside the transaction: if the write fails the row rolls back, and if the commit fails we clean up below.
      if (key && image) {
        await storage.put(key, image.bytes, image.kind.mime);
        wrote = true;
      }
      return created;
    });
    return (await toStampViews(db, me, [row], now))[0]!;
