    await flagContent(prisma, flaggers[0]!, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "Not at this place", category: "NOT_AT_PLACE" });
    await flagContent(prisma, flaggers[1]!, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "just wrong" });
    await flagContent(prisma, flaggers[2]!, { targetType: "USER", targetId: author.handle, reason: "Under 21 or looks underage", category: "UNDERAGE" });
    await flagContent(prisma, flaggers[3]!, { targetType: "USER", targetId: author.handle, reason: "Stalking or unwanted contact", category: "STALKING" });
    expect((await prisma.flag.findMany({ orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" } })).map((f) => f.category)).toEqual(["NOT_AT_PLACE", "OTHER", "UNDERAGE", "STALKING"]);
    await expect(flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "bad category", category: "BORING" })).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it("the queue puts underage and stalking reports first, whatever their age", async () => {
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    const old = await publicStamp();
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: old.stamp.id, reason: "spam spam", category: "SPAM" });
    const urgent = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "USER", targetId: urgent.handle, reason: "Under 21 or looks underage", category: "UNDERAGE" });
    const queue = await moderationQueue(prisma, mod);
    expect(queue.map((q) => q.targetType)).toEqual(["USER", "STAMP"]);
    expect(queue[0]!.flags[0]).toMatchObject({ category: "UNDERAGE" });
    expect(queue[1]!.flags[0]).toMatchObject({ category: "SPAM" });
  });

  it("the old content types are not accepted", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    for (const targetType of ["PHOTO", "COMMENT", "REPORT"]) {
      await expect(flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType, targetId: stamp.id, reason: "nope nope" })).rejects.toThrow();
    }
  });

  it("the hourly flag limit holds under parallel requests", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const flagger = await makeActor();
    const stamps = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 25 }, async () => makeStamp(await makeActor({ isPrivate: false }), venue.id)));
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(stamps.map((s) => flagContent(prisma, flagger, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: s.id, reason: "spam!" })));
    expect(results.filter((r) => r.status === "fulfilled")).toHaveLength(20);
    expect(await prisma.flag.count({ where: { flaggerId: flagger.userId } })).toBe(20);
  });

  it("flags over HTTP: a signed-in person can report a visible stamp", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    const { token } = await createSession(prisma, (await makeActor()).userId);
    const res = await flagRoute(
      new Request("http://localhost/api/flags", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${token}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "not at this place" }) }),
      undefined as never,
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
  });
});

describe("rule 4: hidden content is hidden, never deleted; the log is append-only", () => {
  it("moderator hide keeps the row, resolves flags and logs; restore brings it back", async () => {
    const { stamp, author } = await publicStamp();
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "abusive" });

    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "HIDE", note: "abusive" });
    const hidden = await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } });
    expect(hidden.hiddenAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(hidden.caption).toBe("crowd");
    expect((await listUserStamps(prisma, await makeActor(), author.handle)).stamps).toHaveLength(0);
    // Staff can still open it.
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, mod, stamp.id)).resolves.toMatchObject({ stamp: { hidden: true } });
    expect(await prisma.flag.count({ where: { targetId: stamp.id, status: "OPEN" } })).toBe(0);
    expect(await moderationQueue(prisma, mod)).toHaveLength(0);
    expect((await recentlyHidden(prisma, mod)).map((i) => i.targetId)).toContain(stamp.id);

    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "RESTORE", note: "context" });
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).hiddenAt).toBeNull();
    const log = await prisma.moderationEvent.findMany({ where: { targetId: stamp.id }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" } });
    expect(log.map((e) => e.action)).toEqual(["FLAGGED", "HIDE", "RESTORE"]);
    expect(log[1]).toMatchObject({ actorId: mod.userId, note: "abusive" });
  });

  it("dismissing flags on an auto-hidden stamp restores it, and the whole history is kept", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "fake" });
    const queue = await moderationQueue(prisma, mod);
    expect(queue[0]).toMatchObject({ targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id });
    expect(queue[0]!.content?.hiddenAt).not.toBeNull();
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "DISMISS" });
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).hiddenAt).toBeNull();
    const actions = (await prisma.moderationEvent.findMany({ where: { targetId: stamp.id }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" } })).map((e) => e.action);
    expect(actions).toEqual(["FLAGGED", "FLAGGED", "FLAGGED", "AUTO_HIDE", "DISMISS", "RESTORE"]);
  });

  it("DISMISS does not undo a moderator's own HIDE; RESTORE does", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "HIDE" });
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "DISMISS" });
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).hiddenAt).not.toBeNull();
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "RESTORE" });
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).hiddenAt).toBeNull();
  });

  it("after a dismissal the same user can flag the stamp again", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    const flagger = await makeActor();
    await flagContent(prisma, flagger, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "meh!" });
    await moderate(prisma, mod, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "DISMISS" });
    await expect(flagContent(prisma, flagger, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "still bad" })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("the moderation log can't be edited or deleted, even directly", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "spam!" });
    const ev = await prisma.moderationEvent.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { targetId: stamp.id } });
    await expect(prisma.moderationEvent.update({ where: { id: ev.id }, data: { note: "nothing happened" } })).rejects.toThrow(/append-only/);
    await expect(prisma.moderationEvent.deleteMany({})).rejects.toThrow(/append-only/);
  });

  it("only moderators can see the queue or act; over HTTP too", async () => {
    const { stamp } = await publicStamp();
    await expect(moderationQueue(prisma, await makeActor())).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    await expect(moderationQueue(prisma, await makeActor({ role: "ADMIN" }))).resolves.toEqual([]);
    await expect(moderate(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "HIDE" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    const { token } = await createSession(prisma, (await makeActor()).userId);
    const res = await modRoute(
      new Request("http://localhost/api/mod/actions", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${token}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, action: "HIDE" }) }),
      undefined as never,
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stamp.id } })).hiddenAt).toBeNull();
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/osm-import.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import type { OverpassElement } from "@/lib/osm";
import { importOsmVenues } from "@/server/services/osm-import";
import { GET as placesRoute } from "@/app/api/places/route";

const sample = (JSON.parse(readFileSync("tests/fixtures/overpass-sample.json", "utf8")) as { elements: OverpassElement[] }).elements;

describe("venues:import", () => {
  it("creates places with the default geofence, and is idempotent", async () => {
    expect(await importOsmVenues(prisma, sample)).toMatchObject({ fetched: 4, created: 4, updated: 0 });
    expect(await importOsmVenues(prisma, sample)).toMatchObject({ created: 0, updated: 0, unchanged: 4 });
    const v = await prisma.venue.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { osmId: "node/1001" } });
    expect(v).toMatchObject({ slug: "test-tavern-n1001", geofenceMeters: 100 });
    expect((await prisma.venue.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { osmId: "way/2001" } })).slug).toBe("test-tavern-w2001");
  });

  it("slugs don't change on re-import even if names change; coordinates and names follow OSM", async () => {
    await importOsmVenues(prisma, sample);
    const renamed = sample.map((e) => (e.id === 1001 ? { ...e, lat: 40.7266, tags: { ...e.tags, name: "Renamed In OSM" } } : e.id === 1002 ? { ...e, tags: { ...e.tags, name: "Basement Club NYC" } } : e));
    const r = await importOsmVenues(prisma, renamed);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ updated: 2 });
    expect(await prisma.venue.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { osmId: "node/1001" } })).toMatchObject({ name: "Renamed In OSM", slug: "test-tavern-n1001", lat: 40.7266 });
    expect(await prisma.venue.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { osmId: "node/1002" } })).toMatchObject({ name: "Basement Club NYC", slug: "basement-club" });
  });

  it("adds ODbL attribution to the places API", async () => {
    await importOsmVenues(prisma, sample);
    const body = await (await placesRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/places"), undefined as never)).json();
    expect(body.attribution).toEqual({ text: "© OpenStreetMap contributors", url: "https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright", license: "ODbL-1.0" });
    expect(body.places.every((v: { fromOsm: boolean }) => v.fromOsm)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("recomputes the neighborhood when coordinates move, and rolls back on failure", async () => {
    await importOsmVenues(prisma, sample);
    const moved = sample.map((e) => (e.id === 1001 ? { ...e, lat: 40.7185, lon: -73.988 } : e));
    await importOsmVenues(prisma, moved);
    expect((await prisma.venue.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { osmId: "node/1001" } })).neighborhood).toBe("Lower East Side");
    // A slug clash with an existing place makes the whole import fail with nothing written.
    await prisma.venue.create({ data: { slug: "new-bar-n7777", name: "Squatter", address: "", neighborhood: "East Village", lat: 40.72, lng: -73.98 } });
    const before = await prisma.venue.count();
    const clash: OverpassElement[] = [...sample, { type: "node", id: 7777, lat: 40.7262, lon: -73.9848, tags: { amenity: "bar", name: "New Bar" } }, { type: "node", id: 7778, lat: 40.7263, lon: -73.9849, tags: { amenity: "pub", name: "Brand New" } }, { type: "node", id: 7779, lat: 40.7201, lon: -73.9880, tags: { amenity: "bar", name: "New Bar" } }];
    await expect(importOsmVenues(prisma, clash)).rejects.toThrow(/Slug collision/);
    expect(await prisma.venue.count()).toBe(before);
  });

  it("no attribution when no place came from OSM", async () => {
    await prisma.venue.create({ data: { slug: "manual", name: "Manual", address: "1 St", neighborhood: "East Village", lat: 40.72, lng: -73.98 } });
    const body = await (await placesRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/places"), undefined as never)).json();
    expect(body.attribution).toBeNull();
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/privacy.test.ts
====================================================================================================

// Phase 1: privacy foundation. The same private author is read through every payload by every kind of viewer.
// Stamps and places of a private account reach only people with full access (the author, an approved follower, staff).
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { createStamp, getStamp, listFeed, listUserPlaces, listUserStamps } from "@/server/services/stamps";
import { decideFollowRequest, follow, getProfile, listConnections, searchUsers, updateProfile } from "@/server/services/people";
import { blockUser } from "@/server/services/blocks";
import { likeStamp } from "@/server/services/likes";
import { flagContent } from "@/server/services/moderation";
import { listActivity } from "@/server/services/activity";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { installMemoryStorage } from "./mem-storage";
import { makeActor, makeStamp, makeVenue } from "./factories";

installMemoryStorage();
const wire = (x: unknown) => JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x)));

type Kind = "signed out" | "stranger" | "requester" | "follower" | "self" | "staff" | "blocked by author" | "blocker of author";
const FULL: Kind[] = ["follower", "self", "staff"];

async function scene(isPrivate: boolean) {
  const venue = await makeVenue();
  const author = await makeActor({ isPrivate, handle: `author_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}` });
  const stamp = await makeStamp(author, venue.id, { caption: "secret caption", meta: { songTitle: "Secret Song", songArtist: "Hidden Band" } });
  const follower = await makeActor();
  await follow(prisma, follower, author.handle);
  if (isPrivate) await decideFollowRequest(prisma, author, follower.handle, { approve: true });
  const requester = await makeActor();
  if (isPrivate) await follow(prisma, requester, author.handle);
  const blockedByAuthor = await makeActor();
  await blockUser(prisma, author, blockedByAuthor.handle);
  const blocker = await makeActor();
  await blockUser(prisma, blocker, author.handle);
  const viewers: Record<Kind, Actor | null> = {
    "signed out": null,
    stranger: await makeActor(),
    requester,
    follower,
    self: author,
    staff: await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" }),
    "blocked by author": blockedByAuthor,
    "blocker of author": blocker,
  };
  return { venue, author, stamp, viewers };
}

/** Whether a viewer is expected to see the stamps of this author. Public: everyone except the blocked pair (staff too). */
const sees = (kind: Kind, isPrivate: boolean) => (isPrivate ? FULL.includes(kind) : kind !== "blocked by author" && kind !== "blocker of author");

for (const isPrivate of [true, false]) {
  describe(`an ${isPrivate ? "private" : "public"} account's stamps and places, by viewer`, () => {
    for (const kind of ["signed out", "stranger", "requester", "follower", "self", "staff", "blocked by author", "blocker of author"] as Kind[]) {
      const allowed = sees(kind, isPrivate) || (kind.startsWith("blocked") || kind.startsWith("blocker") ? false : false);
      const staffOverBlock = kind === "staff";
      it(`${kind}: ${allowed || staffOverBlock ? "sees" : "does not see"} the stamp, the grid, the places and the counts`, async () => {
        const { venue, author, stamp, viewers } = await scene(isPrivate);
        const v = viewers[kind];
        const secrets = [stamp.id, venue.slug, venue.name, "secret caption", "Secret Song", "Hidden Band"];
        const leaks = (payload: unknown) => secrets.filter((s) => wire(payload).includes(s));

        // Profile: counts and everything else only with full access; the limited view has the handle and badge only.
        const blockedPair = kind.startsWith("blocked") || kind.startsWith("blocker");
        if (blockedPair) {
          await expect(getProfile(prisma, author.handle, v)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
        } else {
          const profile = await getProfile(prisma, author.handle, v);
          expect(profile.access).toBe(allowed ? "full" : "limited");
          if (profile.access === "limited") expect(Object.keys(profile.user).sort()).toEqual(["handle", "isPrivate", "verified"]);
          expect(leaks(profile)).toEqual([]);
        }

        // Stamp detail: 404 (never 403) so existence isn't revealed.
        if (allowed || staffOverBlock) await expect(getStamp(prisma, v, stamp.id)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
        else await expect(getStamp(prisma, v, stamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });

        // Lists: grid and places.
        for (const list of [() => listUserStamps(prisma, v, author.handle), () => listUserPlaces(prisma, v, author.handle)]) {
          if (allowed || staffOverBlock) await expect(list()).resolves.toBeTruthy();
          else await expect(list()).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: blockedPair ? 404 : 403 });
        }

        // Likes and reports need to see the stamp.
        if (v && !(allowed || staffOverBlock)) {
          await expect(likeStamp(prisma, v, stamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
          await expect(flagContent(prisma, v, { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: stamp.id, reason: "probing around" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
        }

        // Search: the account may be found by handle, but only as the limited view unless the viewer has full access.
        if (v) {
          const found = await searchUsers(prisma, v, author.handle);
          if (blockedPair) expect(found).toEqual([]);
          expect(leaks(found)).toEqual([]);
        }

        // Followers and following lists need full access.
        if (!blockedPair) {
          for (const which of ["followers", "following"] as const) {
            if (allowed) await expect(listConnections(prisma, v, author.handle, which)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
            else await expect(listConnections(prisma, v, author.handle, which)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
          }
        }

        // Feed: only people you follow with approved follows; a pending request shows nothing. Never a blocked pair.
        if (v) {
          const feed = await listFeed(prisma, v);
          const expected = kind === "follower";
          expect(feed.stamps.map((s) => s.id).includes(stamp.id)).toBe(expected);
        }
      });
    }
  });
}

describe("activity never carries a private account's stamps to people without access", () => {
  it("a private person who likes your stamp shows as a handle only; their own stamps never appear", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const liker = await makeActor(); // private
    await makeStamp(liker, venue.id, { caption: "liker's secret", meta: { songTitle: "Liker Song", songArtist: "Liker Band" } });
    const mine = await makeStamp(me, (await makeVenue()).id);
    await prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: mine.id }, data: {} }).catch(() => {});
    await likeStamp(prisma, liker, mine.id);
    await follow(prisma, liker, me.handle);
    const activity = await listActivity(prisma, me);
    const json = wire(activity);
    expect(activity.find((a) => a.kind === "like")).toMatchObject({ person: { handle: liker.handle, displayName: null, avatarUrl: null } });
    expect(json).not.toContain("liker's secret");
    expect(json).not.toContain("Liker Song");
    expect(json).not.toContain(venue.slug);
  });

  it("likes by or on a blocked person disappear from activity in both directions", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const fan = await makeActor();
    const mine = await makeStamp(me, (await makeVenue()).id);
    await likeStamp(prisma, fan, mine.id);
    expect((await listActivity(prisma, me)).filter((a) => a.kind === "like")).toHaveLength(1);
    await blockUser(prisma, me, fan.handle);
    expect((await listActivity(prisma, me)).filter((a) => a.kind === "like")).toHaveLength(0);
    expect((await listActivity(prisma, fan)).filter((a) => a.kind === "like")).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

describe("blocking by handle hides the pair from each other everywhere", () => {
  it("profile, search, lists, feed, activity and likes: nothing leaks in either direction", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const [a, b] = [await makeActor({ isPrivate: false }), await makeActor({ isPrivate: false })];
    const sa = await makeStamp(a, venue.id, { caption: "a's stamp" });
    const sb = await makeStamp(b, (await makeVenue()).id, { caption: "b's stamp" });
    await follow(prisma, a, b.handle);
    await follow(prisma, b, a.handle);
    await likeStamp(prisma, a, sb.id);
    await likeStamp(prisma, b, sa.id);
    await blockUser(prisma, a, b.handle);
    for (const [x, y, ys] of [[a, b, sb], [b, a, sa]] as const) {
      await expect(getProfile(prisma, y.handle, x)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
      await expect(getStamp(prisma, x, ys.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
      await expect(listUserStamps(prisma, x, y.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
      await expect(listUserPlaces(prisma, x, y.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
      await expect(listConnections(prisma, x, y.handle, "followers")).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
      await expect(likeStamp(prisma, x, ys.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
      await expect(follow(prisma, x, y.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
      expect(await searchUsers(prisma, x, y.handle)).toEqual([]);
      expect((await listFeed(prisma, x)).stamps).toEqual([]);
      expect(wire(await listActivity(prisma, x))).not.toContain(y.handle);
    }
    // Neither side's like counts on the other's stamp are shown to the blocked party; staff still see both.
    const staff = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, staff, sa.id)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    await expect(getProfile(prisma, b.handle, staff)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("a third party sees neither person's block", async () => {
    const [a, b, c] = [await makeActor({ isPrivate: false }), await makeActor({ isPrivate: false }), await makeActor()];
    await blockUser(prisma, a, b.handle);
    const profile = await getProfile(prisma, a.handle, c);
    expect(wire(profile)).not.toContain(b.handle);
  });
});

describe("going public changes what strangers can see", () => {
  it("every stamp and place becomes visible to strangers and signed-out people; going private hides them again", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const author = await makeActor();
    const stamp = await makeStamp(author, venue.id);
    const stranger = await makeActor();
    await expect(listUserStamps(prisma, stranger, author.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    await expect(listUserPlaces(prisma, null, author.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    await updateProfile(prisma, author, { isPrivate: false });
    expect((await listUserStamps(prisma, stranger, author.handle)).stamps.map((s) => s.id)).toEqual([stamp.id]);
    expect((await listUserPlaces(prisma, null, author.handle)).places).toHaveLength(1);
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, null, stamp.id)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    await updateProfile(prisma, author, { isPrivate: true });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, stranger, stamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(listUserStamps(prisma, null, author.handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
  });
});

describe("per-stamp visibility is the account's privacy: there is no sharing toggle", () => {
  it("a stamp request that tries to set `shared` is refused, and the schema has no such column", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    await expect(createStamp(prisma, me, venue.id, { meta: { caption: "x", lat: "40.7265", lng: "-73.9845", shared: "true" } })).rejects.toThrow();
    const cols = await prisma.$queryRaw<{ column_name: string }[]>`SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_name = 'photo'`;
    expect(cols.map((c) => c.column_name)).not.toContain("shared");
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/seed.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { login } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { getStamp, listFeed, listUserStamps } from "@/server/services/stamps";
import { getProfile } from "@/server/services/people";
import { moderationQueue } from "@/server/services/moderation";
import { getMe } from "@/server/services/me";
import { actorFor } from "./factories";
import { getStorage } from "@/lib/storage";
import { installMemoryStorage } from "./mem-storage";
import { seed } from "../../prisma/seed";
import { PEOPLE, PLACES } from "../../prisma/seed-data";

const mem = installMemoryStorage();
const NOW = new Date("2026-10-02T16:00:00Z");
const env = { APP_ENV: "development", DEV_SEED_PASSWORD: "seed password 123" };
const run = (now = NOW) => seed(prisma, { now, storage: getStorage(), env });
const id = (handle: string) => `seed_u_${handle}`;
const actor = async (handle: string) => (await actorFor(id(handle)))!;

describe("the demo seed", () => {
  it("is deterministic: the same anchor gives the same rows", async () => {
    await run();
    const snap = async () =>
      JSON.stringify([
        await prisma.photo.findMany({ orderBy: { id: "asc" }, select: { id: true, userId: true, venueId: true, caption: true, songTitle: true, storageKey: true, createdAt: true, hiddenAt: true, removedAt: true } }),
        await prisma.photoLike.findMany({ orderBy: [{ userId: "asc" }, { photoId: "asc" }] }),
        await prisma.follow.findMany({ orderBy: [{ followerId: "asc" }, { followeeId: "asc" }] }),
      ]);
    const first = await snap();
    await run();
    expect(await snap()).toBe(first);
  });

  it("is anchored to now: a later anchor moves every timestamp with it", async () => {
    await run();
    const latestA = (await prisma.photo.findFirstOrThrow({ orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" } })).createdAt.getTime();
    await run(new Date(NOW.getTime() + 3 * 86_400_000));
    const latestB = (await prisma.photo.findFirstOrThrow({ orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" } })).createdAt.getTime();
    expect(latestB - latestA).toBe(3 * 86_400_000);
    expect(NOW.getTime() - latestA).toBeLessThan(24 * 3600_000);
  });

  it("has the cast: public and private accounts, one verified, one blocked pair, pending requests, staff and App Review", async () => {
    const s = await run();
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ places: PLACES.length, people: PEOPLE.length });
    const users = await prisma.user.findMany();
    expect(users.some((u) => u.isPrivate) && users.some((u) => !u.isPrivate)).toBe(true);
    expect(users.filter((u) => u.verifiedAt).map((u) => u.handle)).toEqual(["maya_r"]);
    expect(await prisma.verificationEvent.count()).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.block.count()).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.block.findFirstOrThrow()).toMatchObject({ blockerId: id("blocker_blake"), blockedId: id("blocked_bea") });
    expect(await prisma.followRequest.count({ where: { targetId: id("avaa") } })).toBe(4);
    expect(await prisma.follow.count({ where: { followerId: id("moth_hopeful") } })).toBe(0);
    expect(users.find((u) => u.handle === "admin")!.role).toBe("ADMIN");
    expect(users.find((u) => u.handle === "mod_morgan")!.role).toBe("MODERATOR");
    expect(users.find((u) => u.handle === "appreview")!.geofenceExempt).toBe(true);
    expect(users.filter((u) => u.handle !== "appreview").every((u) => !u.geofenceExempt)).toBe(true);
    // No owner accounts, and nobody is "anonymous".
    expect(users.map((u) => u.handle).join(",")).not.toMatch(/owner|anonymous/);
  });

  it("has stamps of every kind at several places, with likes that obey the rules", async () => {
    await run();
    const stamps = await prisma.photo.findMany();
    expect(stamps.length).toBeGreaterThan(40);
    expect(stamps.some((s) => s.storageKey && s.caption && s.songTitle)).toBe(true); // photo + caption + song
    expect(stamps.some((s) => s.storageKey && !s.caption && !s.songTitle)).toBe(true); // photo only
    expect(stamps.some((s) => !s.storageKey && s.caption && !s.songTitle)).toBe(true); // caption only
    expect(stamps.some((s) => !s.storageKey && !s.caption && s.songTitle)).toBe(true); // song only
    expect(new Set(stamps.map((s) => s.venueId)).size).toBeGreaterThan(8);
    for (const s of stamps.filter((x) => x.storageKey)) expect(mem.has(s.storageKey!)).toBe(true);
    const likes = await prisma.photoLike.findMany({ include: { photo: true } });
    expect(likes.length).toBeGreaterThan(50);
    expect(likes.every((l) => l.userId !== l.photo.userId && !l.photo.hiddenAt && !l.photo.removedAt && l.photo.countable)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("every stamp is inside its place's geofence, except App Review's, which only that account can see", async () => {
    await run();
    const places = new Map((await prisma.venue.findMany()).map((p) => [p.id, p]));
    const { distanceMeters } = await import("@/lib/geo");
    for (const s of await prisma.photo.findMany()) {
      const p = places.get(s.venueId)!;
      const d = distanceMeters({ lat: s.lat, lng: s.lng }, p);
      if (s.userId === id("appreview")) {
        expect(s.countable).toBe(false);
        expect(d).toBeGreaterThan(p.geofenceMeters);
      } else expect(d).toBeLessThanOrEqual(p.geofenceMeters);
    }
    const reviewStamp = await prisma.photo.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { userId: id("appreview") } });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, await actor("maya_r"), reviewStamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, await actor("appreview"), reviewStamp.id)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("privacy holds on the seeded data: a stranger sees nothing of a private passport, a follower sees it, a pending request is not enough", async () => {
    await run();
    const priv = "avaa";
    const stranger = await actor("moth_hopeful");
    const follower = await actor("maya_r");
    const requester = await actor("rowan_k");
    for (const v of [null, stranger, requester]) {
      expect((await getProfile(prisma, priv, v)).access).toBe("limited");
      await expect(listUserStamps(prisma, v, priv)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    }
    expect((await getProfile(prisma, priv, follower)).access).toBe("full");
    const stamp = await prisma.photo.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { userId: id(priv), hiddenAt: null, removedAt: null } });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, stranger, stamp.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, follower, stamp.id)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    expect((await listFeed(prisma, await actor("avaa"))).stamps.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("the blocked pair cannot see each other; a hidden and a removed stamp are gone for everyone but staff", async () => {
    await run();
    const blake = await actor("blocker_blake");
    const bea = await actor("blocked_bea");
    await expect(getProfile(prisma, "blocked_bea", blake)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(getProfile(prisma, "blocker_blake", bea)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    const hidden = await prisma.photo.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { hiddenAt: { not: null } } });
    const removed = await prisma.photo.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { removedAt: { not: null } } });
    const viewer = await actor("nia_s");
    for (const s of [hidden, removed]) {
      await expect(getStamp(prisma, viewer, s.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
      await expect(getStamp(prisma, await actor("mod_morgan"), s.id)).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    }
  });

  it("gives the moderator a queue to try, and the demo accounts sign in with the dev password", async () => {
    await run();
    expect((await moderationQueue(prisma, await actor("mod_morgan"))).length).toBe(1);
    expect((await getMe(prisma, await actor("avaa"))).social.pendingFollowRequests).toBe(4);
    for (const handle of ["avaa", "maya_r", "mod_morgan", "admin", "appreview", "moth_hopeful"]) {
      expect(await login(prisma, { email: `${handle}@lastcall.dev`, password: "seed password 123" })).toBeTruthy();
    }
  });

  it("refuses to run against production", async () => {
    await expect(seed(prisma, { now: NOW, storage: getStorage(), env: { APP_ENV: "production" } })).rejects.toThrow(/Refusing to run the demo seed/);
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/serverless.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { login, signup, pruneLoginAttempts } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { GET as cronGet, POST as cronPost } from "@/app/api/cron/maintenance/route";
import { GET as health } from "@/app/api/health/route";
import { GET as uploads } from "@/app/api/uploads/[...key]/route";
import { installMemoryStorage } from "./mem-storage";
import { getStorage } from "@/lib/storage";

const mem = installMemoryStorage();

describe("login throttle (Postgres, email + IP)", () => {
  it("locks one IP after 10 failures without locking the same account from another IP", async () => {
    await signup(prisma, { email: "t@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "T", handle: "ttt", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) await login(prisma, { email: "t@x.dev", password: "nope" }, { ip: "203.0.113.9" }).catch(() => {});
    await expect(login(prisma, { email: "t@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" }, { ip: "203.0.113.9" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429 });
    await expect(login(prisma, { email: "t@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" }, { ip: "198.51.100.1" })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    expect(await prisma.loginAttempt.count({ where: { ip: "203.0.113.9" } })).toBe(10);
  });

  it("expires after 15 minutes and clears on success", async () => {
    await signup(prisma, { email: "u@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "U", handle: "uuu", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    const t0 = new Date();
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) await login(prisma, { email: "u@x.dev", password: "nope" }, { ip: "1.1.1.1" }, t0).catch(() => {});
    const later = new Date(t0.getTime() + 15 * 60_000 + 1);
    await login(prisma, { email: "u@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" }, { ip: "1.1.1.1" }, later);
    expect(await prisma.loginAttempt.count({ where: { email: "u@x.dev" } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it("can't be raced: parallel wrong guesses stop at the limit", async () => {
    await signup(prisma, { email: "r@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "R", handle: "rrr", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(Array.from({ length: 25 }, () => login(prisma, { email: "r@x.dev", password: "nope" }, { ip: "9.9.9.9" })));
    expect(results.filter((r) => r.status === "rejected" && (r.reason as { status: number }).status === 401)).toHaveLength(10);
    expect(await prisma.loginAttempt.count({ where: { email: "r@x.dev" } })).toBe(10);
  });

  it("caps failures per IP across many emails", async () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) await prisma.loginAttempt.create({ data: { email: `spray${i}@x.dev`, ip: "6.6.6.6" } });
    await expect(login(prisma, { email: "fresh@x.dev", password: "x" }, { ip: "6.6.6.6" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429 });
    await expect(login(prisma, { email: "not-an-email", password: "x" }, { ip: "7.7.7.7" })).rejects.toThrow();
    expect(await prisma.loginAttempt.count({ where: { ip: "7.7.7.7" } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it("prunes day-old attempts", async () => {
    await prisma.loginAttempt.create({ data: { email: "a@x", ip: "1", createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 2 * 86_400_000) } });
    await prisma.loginAttempt.create({ data: { email: "a@x", ip: "1" } });
    expect(await pruneLoginAttempts(prisma)).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe("cron route", () => {
  const withSecret = (h?: string) => new Request("http://x/api/cron/maintenance", { headers: h ? { authorization: h } : {} });
  it("rejects missing or wrong secrets and runs with the right one (GET for Vercel Cron, POST manually)", async () => {
    process.env.CRON_SECRET = "test-cron-secret-123456";
    expect((await cronGet(withSecret())).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await cronGet(withSecret("Bearer wrong"))).status).toBe(401);
    const res = await cronGet(withSecret("Bearer test-cron-secret-123456"));
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await res.json()).toMatchObject({ ok: true, prunedLoginAttempts: expect.any(Number), prunedRateEvents: expect.any(Number) });
    expect((await cronPost(withSecret("Bearer test-cron-secret-123456"))).status).toBe(200);
  });
});

describe("health", () => {
  it("reports the database as up", async () => {
    const res = await health();
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await res.json()).toMatchObject({ ok: true, db: "up" });
  });
});

describe("upload route requires a valid signature", () => {
  it("serves signed URLs only", async () => {
    mem.set("photos/aaaa.png", Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50]));
    const signed = await getStorage().signedUrl("photos/aaaa.png");
    const call = (u: string) => uploads(new Request(`http://x${u}`), { params: Promise.resolve({ key: new URL(u, "http://x").pathname.replace("/api/uploads/", "").split("/") }) });
    expect((await call(signed)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await call("/api/uploads/photos/aaaa.png")).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await call(signed.replace(/sig=[^&]+/, "sig=forged"))).status).toBe(404);
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/setup.ts
====================================================================================================

import "./env";
import { afterAll, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";

const TABLES = [
  "rate_event", "verification_event", "photo_like", "follow_request", "block", "login_attempt",
  "moderation_event", "flag", "follow", "photo", "venue", "session", "app_user",
];

beforeEach(async () => {
  // TRUNCATE does not fire the row-level no-delete triggers, so tests start from a clean slate.
  await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`TRUNCATE TABLE ${TABLES.map((t) => `"${t}"`).join(", ")} RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE`);
});

afterAll(async () => {
  await prisma.$disconnect();
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/songs.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { ITUNES_SEARCH_URL, SONG_RESULTS, SONG_SEARCH_TIMEOUT_MS, searchSongs } from "@/server/services/songs";
import { RATE_LIMITS } from "@/server/rate-limit";
import { makeActor } from "./factories";

const track = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  trackId: 1, trackName: "Heroes", artistName: "David Bowie", collectionName: "Heroes", artworkUrl100: "https://is1-ssl.mzstatic.com/image/thumb/Music/a/b/100x100bb.jpg", ...over,
});
const reply = (results: unknown[]): typeof fetch => (async () => new Response(JSON.stringify({ resultCount: results.length, results }), { status: 200 })) as typeof fetch;

describe("song search (proxied iTunes Search API)", () => {
  it("returns metadata only, with art upgraded to a larger rendition on Apple's CDN", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor();
    const out = await searchSongs(prisma, me, "heroes", new Date(), reply([track()]));
    expect(out).toEqual({ songs: [{ title: "Heroes", artist: "David Bowie", album: "Heroes", artUrl: "https://is1-ssl.mzstatic.com/image/thumb/Music/a/b/300x300bb.jpg", sourceId: "1" }] });
  });

  it("sends nothing about the user upstream: only the term, a fixed country and limit, a short timeout, no credentials", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor();
    let seen: { url: URL; init: RequestInit } | null = null;
    const spy = (async (input: URL, init: RequestInit) => {
      seen = { url: input, init };
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ results: [] }));
    }) as unknown as typeof fetch;
    await searchSongs(prisma, me, "  bowie  ", new Date(), spy);
    expect(seen!.url.origin + seen!.url.pathname).toBe(ITUNES_SEARCH_URL);
    expect([...seen!.url.searchParams.keys()].sort()).toEqual(["country", "entity", "limit", "media", "term"]);
    expect(seen!.url.searchParams.get("term")).toBe("bowie");
    expect(seen!.url.searchParams.get("limit")).toBe(String(SONG_RESULTS));
    expect(seen!.init.credentials).toBe("omit");
    expect(Object.keys(seen!.init.headers as object)).toEqual(["accept"]);
    expect(seen!.init.signal).toBeInstanceOf(AbortSignal);
    expect(JSON.stringify([...seen!.url.searchParams, seen!.init.headers])).not.toContain(me.userId);
    expect(JSON.stringify([...seen!.url.searchParams, seen!.init.headers])).not.toContain(me.handle);
    expect(SONG_SEARCH_TIMEOUT_MS).toBeLessThanOrEqual(5000);
  });

  it("drops results with objectionable text, non-Apple art, or oversized fields", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor();
    const out = await searchSongs(prisma, me, "x1", new Date(), reply([
      track({ trackId: 1 }), track({ trackId: 2, trackName: "porn night" }), track({ trackId: 3, artistName: "f4gg0t" }),
      track({ trackId: 4, artworkUrl100: "https://evil.example/pixel.gif" }), track({ trackId: 5, trackName: "x".repeat(201) }),
    ]));
    expect(out.songs.map((s) => [s.sourceId, s.artUrl === null])).toEqual([["1", false], ["4", true]]);
  });

  it("needs a signed-in user and a sensible query, and counts against a per-user rate limit", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor();
    const f = reply([]);
    await expect(searchSongs(prisma, null, "bowie", new Date(), f)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
    await expect(searchSongs(prisma, me, "a", new Date(), f)).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(searchSongs(prisma, me, "x".repeat(81), new Date(), f)).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(searchSongs(prisma, me, undefined, new Date(), f)).rejects.toThrow();
    const t = new Date();
    for (let i = 0; i < RATE_LIMITS.song_search.max; i++) await prisma.rateEvent.create({ data: { userId: me.userId, kind: "song_search", createdAt: t } });
    await expect(searchSongs(prisma, me, "bowie", t, f)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429 });
  });

  it("answers a clear error when Apple is slow, down or returns junk", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor();
    const down = (async () => new Response("nope", { status: 503 })) as typeof fetch;
    const throws = (async () => { throw new Error("timeout"); }) as typeof fetch;
    const junk = (async () => new Response(JSON.stringify({ results: [{ nonsense: true }] }))) as typeof fetch;
    await expect(searchSongs(prisma, me, "bowie", new Date(), down)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, code: "song_search_unavailable" });
    await expect(searchSongs(prisma, me, "bowie", new Date(), throws)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "song_search_unavailable" });
    await expect(searchSongs(prisma, me, "bowie", new Date(), junk)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400 });
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/stamps.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { createStamp, editStamp, getStamp, listFeed, listUserPlaces, listUserStamps, removeStamp, STAMPS_PAGE } from "@/server/services/stamps";
import { likeStamp, unlikeStamp } from "@/server/services/likes";
import { flagContent } from "@/server/services/moderation";
import { follow } from "@/server/services/people";
import { nearbyPlaces } from "@/server/services/places";
import { RATE_LIMITS } from "@/server/rate-limit";
import { installMemoryStorage } from "./mem-storage";
import { makeActor, makeStamp, makeVenue, INSIDE, OUTSIDE } from "./factories";

const mem = installMemoryStorage();
const ART = "https://is1-ssl.mzstatic.com/image/thumb/Music/x/300x300bb.jpg";

describe("creating a stamp", () => {
  it("takes a photo, a caption, a song, or any mix, and refuses an empty one", async () => {
    const [a, b, c, d] = [await makeVenue(), await makeVenue(), await makeVenue(), await makeVenue()];
    const me = await makeActor();
    const photoOnly = await makeStamp(me, a.id, { caption: "", photo: true });
    expect(photoOnly).toMatchObject({ caption: "", song: null });
    expect(photoOnly.photoUrl).toMatch(/^\/api\/uploads\/photos\//);
    const captionOnly = await makeStamp(me, b.id, { caption: "just words", photo: false });
    expect(captionOnly).toMatchObject({ photoUrl: null, caption: "just words", song: null });
    const songOnly = await makeStamp(me, c.id, { caption: "", photo: false, meta: { songTitle: "Wild Horses", songArtist: "The Rolling Stones", songArtUrl: ART, songSourceId: "42" } });
    expect(songOnly).toMatchObject({ photoUrl: null, caption: "", song: { title: "Wild Horses", artist: "The Rolling Stones", artUrl: ART, sourceId: "42" } });
    await expect(makeStamp(me, d.id, { caption: "", photo: false })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400, message: expect.stringMatching(/photo, a caption or a song/) });
    await expect(makeStamp(me, d.id, { caption: "   ", photo: false })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400 });
    expect(await prisma.photo.count({ where: { venueId: d.id } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it("validates captions and songs", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    await expect(makeStamp(me, v.id, { caption: "x".repeat(281) })).rejects.toThrow(/280 characters max/);
    await expect(makeStamp(me, v.id, { caption: "x", meta: { songTitle: "Only a title" } })).rejects.toThrow(/title and an artist/);
    await expect(makeStamp(me, v.id, { caption: "x", meta: { songTitle: "T", songArtist: "A", songArtUrl: "https://evil.example/pixel.gif" } })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(makeStamp(me, v.id, { caption: "x", meta: { songTitle: "T", songArtist: "A", songArtUrl: "http://a.mzstatic.com/x.jpg" } })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(makeStamp(me, v.id, { caption: "x", meta: { rating: "11" } })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(makeStamp(me, v.id, { caption: "x", meta: { bogus: "1" } })).rejects.toThrow();
    expect(await prisma.photo.count()).toBe(0);
    // The optional rating is stored but never shown.
    const ok = await makeStamp(me, v.id, { caption: "rated", meta: { rating: "9" } });
    expect(JSON.stringify(ok)).not.toMatch(/rating/);
    expect((await prisma.photo.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: ok.id } })).rating).toBe(9);
  });

  it("is geofenced per place: the place's own radius decides, the message says how far away you are", async () => {
    const tight = await makeVenue({ geofenceMeters: 30 });
    const wide = await makeVenue({ geofenceMeters: 400 });
    const me = await makeActor();
    const near = { lat: INSIDE.lat + 0.0006, lng: INSIDE.lng }; // roughly 87 m from the place
    await expect(makeStamp(me, tight.id, { at: near })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, message: expect.stringMatching(/within 30 m.*you're \d+ m away/) });
    await expect(makeStamp(me, wide.id, { at: near })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    await expect(makeStamp(me, wide.id, { at: { lat: 91, lng: 0 }, now: new Date(Date.now() + 20 * 60_000) })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400 });
    await expect(makeStamp(me, "no-such-place", {})).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(createStamp(prisma, null, wide.id, { meta: { caption: "x", lat: "1", lng: "1" } })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
  });

  it("allows one stamp per place per 15 minutes, and 20 an hour across places", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    const t = new Date();
    await makeStamp(me, v.id, { now: t });
    await expect(makeStamp(me, v.id, { now: new Date(t.getTime() + 5 * 60_000) })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429 });
    await expect(makeStamp(me, v.id, { now: new Date(t.getTime() + 16 * 60_000) })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    const other = await makeActor();
    for (let i = 0; i < RATE_LIMITS.post.max; i++) await makeStamp(other, (await makeVenue()).id, { now: t });
    await expect(makeStamp(other, (await makeVenue()).id, { now: t })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429 });
  });

  it("strips EXIF and leaves no photo behind when the transaction fails", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    await makeStamp(me, v.id);
    const before = mem.size;
    await expect(makeStamp(me, v.id, { now: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000) })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429 });
    expect(mem.size).toBe(before);
  });

  it("rejects things that aren't images", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    await expect(createStamp(prisma, me, v.id, { bytes: Buffer.from("not an image at all"), meta: { caption: "x", lat: String(INSIDE.lat), lng: String(INSIDE.lng) } })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 415 });
  });

  it("an account with no stamps has no places, and a stamp's response never carries coordinates or storage keys", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    const s = await makeStamp(me, v.id);
    expect(JSON.stringify(s)).not.toMatch(/"(lat|lng|storageKey|userId)"/);
    expect(s.author).toEqual({ handle: me.handle, displayName: me.displayName, avatarUrl: null, verified: false });
  });
});

describe("editing and removing a stamp", () => {
  it("the author edits the caption and the song; the photo, place and time never change", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    const s = await makeStamp(me, v.id, { caption: "before" });
    const edited = await editStamp(prisma, me, s.id, { caption: "after", song: { title: "Song", artist: "Band", artUrl: ART } });
    expect(edited.stamp).toMatchObject({ caption: "after", song: { title: "Song", artist: "Band", artUrl: ART, sourceId: null }, photoUrl: s.photoUrl, createdAt: s.createdAt });
    expect(edited.stamp.editedAt).not.toBeNull();
    const cleared = await editStamp(prisma, me, s.id, { song: null });
    expect(cleared.stamp.song).toBeNull();
    expect(cleared.stamp.caption).toBe("after");
    await expect(editStamp(prisma, me, s.id, {})).rejects.toThrow(/Nothing to change/);
    await expect(editStamp(prisma, me, s.id, { caption: "x".repeat(281) })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(editStamp(prisma, me, s.id, { caption: "porn" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "objectionable_content" });
    await expect(editStamp(prisma, me, s.id, { song: { title: "T", artist: "A", artUrl: "https://evil.example/x.gif" } })).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it("a stamp with no photo can't be edited into nothing", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor();
    const s = await makeStamp(me, v.id, { caption: "words", photo: false });
    await expect(editStamp(prisma, me, s.id, { caption: "" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400 });
    // A photo stamp can lose its caption.
    const w = await makeVenue();
    const p = await makeStamp(me, w.id, { caption: "words" });
    await expect(editStamp(prisma, me, p.id, { caption: "" })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("only the author edits or removes: everyone else, staff included, gets 403 or 404", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const s = await makeStamp(me, v.id);
    for (const other of [await makeActor(), await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" }), await makeActor({ role: "ADMIN" })]) {
      await expect(editStamp(prisma, other, s.id, { caption: "mine now" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
      await expect(removeStamp(prisma, other, s.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    }
    await expect(editStamp(prisma, null, s.id, { caption: "x" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
  });

  it("editing freezes while a flag is open and after a moderator hides it", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const s = await makeStamp(me, v.id);
    await flagContent(prisma, await makeActor(), { targetType: "STAMP", targetId: s.id, reason: "looks fake" });
    await expect(editStamp(prisma, me, s.id, { caption: "rewrite" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 409 });
    await prisma.flag.updateMany({ data: { status: "RESOLVED" } });
    await expect(editStamp(prisma, me, s.id, { caption: "ok now" })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
    await prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: s.id }, data: { hiddenAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(editStamp(prisma, me, s.id, { caption: "again" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 409 });
  });

  it("'delete from profile' hides it everywhere for good, and never deletes the row", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const viewer = await makeActor();
    await follow(prisma, viewer, me.handle);
    const s = await makeStamp(me, v.id);
    await removeStamp(prisma, me, s.id);
    await expect(removeStamp(prisma, me, s.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, viewer, s.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, me, s.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    expect((await listUserStamps(prisma, me, me.handle)).stamps).toEqual([]);
    expect((await listFeed(prisma, viewer)).stamps).toEqual([]);
    expect((await listUserPlaces(prisma, me, me.handle)).places).toEqual([]);
    await expect(editStamp(prisma, me, s.id, { caption: "back?" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    // Staff can still see it (moderation), and the row is still there.
    await expect(getStamp(prisma, await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" }), s.id)).resolves.toMatchObject({ stamp: { removed: true } });
    expect(await prisma.photo.count({ where: { id: s.id } })).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe("likes", () => {
  it("one per person, not your own, counted once, undoable", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const author = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const s = await makeStamp(author, v.id);
    const fan = await makeActor();
    await expect(likeStamp(prisma, author, s.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400 });
    expect(await likeStamp(prisma, fan, s.id)).toEqual({ liked: true, count: 1, viewerLiked: true });
    expect(await likeStamp(prisma, fan, s.id)).toEqual({ liked: true, count: 1, viewerLiked: true });
    expect((await getStamp(prisma, await makeActor(), s.id)).stamp.likes).toEqual({ count: 1, viewerLiked: false });
    expect(await unlikeStamp(prisma, fan, s.id)).toEqual({ liked: false, count: 0, viewerLiked: false });
    await expect(likeStamp(prisma, null, s.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
  });

  it("not on hidden or removed stamps, and not on stamps you can't see", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const author = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const hidden = await makeStamp(author, v.id);
    const w = await makeVenue();
    const removed = await makeStamp(author, w.id);
    await prisma.photo.update({ where: { id: hidden.id }, data: { hiddenAt: new Date() } });
    await removeStamp(prisma, author, removed.id);
    const fan = await makeActor();
    const mod = await makeActor({ role: "MODERATOR" });
    for (const viewer of [fan, mod]) {
      await expect(likeStamp(prisma, viewer, hidden.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
      await expect(likeStamp(prisma, viewer, removed.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    }
    const priv = await makeActor();
    const x = await makeVenue();
    const secret = await makeStamp(priv, x.id);
    await expect(likeStamp(prisma, fan, secret.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
  });

  it("App Review likes and suspended people's likes don't count", async () => {
    const v = await makeVenue();
    const author = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const s = await makeStamp(author, v.id);
    const reviewer = await makeActor();
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: reviewer.userId }, data: { geofenceExempt: true } });
    await likeStamp(prisma, { ...reviewer, geofenceExempt: true }, s.id);
    const bad = await makeActor();
    await likeStamp(prisma, bad, s.id);
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: bad.userId }, data: { suspendedAt: new Date() } });
    expect((await getStamp(prisma, author, s.id)).stamp.likes.count).toBe(0);
  });

  it("the hourly like limit holds", async () => {
    const author = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const fan = await makeActor();
    const t = new Date();
    for (let i = 0; i < RATE_LIMITS.like.max; i++) {
      await prisma.rateEvent.create({ data: { userId: fan.userId, kind: "like", createdAt: t } });
    }
    const s = await makeStamp(author, (await makeVenue()).id);
    await expect(likeStamp(prisma, fan, s.id, t)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429 });
  });
});

describe("the feed and the passport", () => {
  it("pages 30 at a time with a stable cursor, newest first, with no gaps or repeats", async () => {
    const me = await makeActor();
    const author = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    await follow(prisma, me, author.handle);
    const base = Date.now() - 100 * 60_000;
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    // Two stamps in the same millisecond (cooldown bypassed by direct inserts) to prove ties don't skip rows.
    const same = new Date(base + 5000 * 60);
    const ids: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < STAMPS_PAGE + 5; i++) {
      const row = await prisma.photo.create({ data: { userId: author.userId, venueId: venue.id, caption: `s${i}`, ...INSIDE, createdAt: i >= STAMPS_PAGE ? same : new Date(base + i * 1000) } });
      ids.push(row.id);
    }
    const first = await listFeed(prisma, me);
    expect(first.stamps).toHaveLength(STAMPS_PAGE);
    expect(first.nextBefore).not.toBeNull();
    const second = await listFeed(prisma, me, first.nextBefore);
    const all = [...first.stamps, ...second.stamps].map((s) => s.id);
    expect(second.nextBefore).toBeNull();
    expect(new Set(all).size).toBe(STAMPS_PAGE + 5);
    expect([...all].sort()).toEqual([...ids].sort());
    const times = [...first.stamps, ...second.stamps].map((s) => s.createdAt.getTime());
    expect([...times].sort((a, b) => b - a)).toEqual(times);
    await expect(listFeed(prisma, me, "garbage")).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400 });
    await expect(listFeed(prisma, null)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
  });

  it("only shows people you follow: not yourself, not strangers, not people you unfollowed", async () => {
    const venue = await makeVenue();
    const me = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const a = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const stranger = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    await makeStamp(me, venue.id, { caption: "mine" });
    await makeStamp(stranger, venue.id, { caption: "stranger" });
    await makeStamp(a, venue.id, { caption: "a" });
    expect((await listFeed(prisma, me)).stamps).toEqual([]);
    await follow(prisma, me, a.handle);
    expect((await listFeed(prisma, me)).stamps.map((s) => s.caption)).toEqual(["a"]);
    await prisma.follow.deleteMany({});
    expect((await listFeed(prisma, me)).stamps).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("the passport grid and places view count and group stamps by place", async () => {
    const [a, b] = [await makeVenue(), await makeVenue()];
    const me = await makeActor({ isPrivate: false });
    const t = Date.now() - 3 * 3600_000;
    await makeStamp(me, a.id, { caption: "first at a", now: new Date(t) });
    await makeStamp(me, a.id, { caption: "second at a", now: new Date(t + 3600_000) });
    const last = await makeStamp(me, b.id, { caption: "at b", now: new Date(t + 2 * 3600_000) });
    const viewer = await makeActor();
    const grid = await listUserStamps(prisma, viewer, me.handle);
    expect(grid.stamps.map((s) => s.caption)).toEqual(["at b", "second at a", "first at a"]);
    const { places } = await listUserPlaces(prisma, viewer, me.handle);
    expect(places.map((p) => [p.place.slug, p.stamps])).toEqual([[b.slug, 1], [a.slug, 2]]);
    expect(places[0]!.latestStamp.id).toBe(last.id);
    expect(places[1]!.latestStamp.caption).toBe("second at a");
  });
});

describe("places", () => {
  it("lists places near a position, nearest first, flagging the ones whose geofence you're inside", async () => {
    const here = await makeVenue({ lat: INSIDE.lat, lng: INSIDE.lng });
    const close = await makeVenue({ lat: INSIDE.lat + 0.002, lng: INSIDE.lng });
    await makeVenue({ lat: OUTSIDE.lat + 0.1, lng: OUTSIDE.lng });
    const near = await nearbyPlaces(prisma, INSIDE);
    expect(near.map((p) => p.slug)).toEqual([here.slug, close.slug]);
    expect(near.map((p) => p.inside)).toEqual([true, false]);
    expect(near[0]!.distanceM).toBe(0);
    expect(JSON.stringify(near)).not.toMatch(/osmId/);
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/integration/token-auth.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { signup } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { hashToken, SESSION_COOKIE } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { POST as loginRoute } from "@/app/api/auth/login/route";
import { POST as signupRoute } from "@/app/api/auth/signup/route";
import { POST as logoutRoute } from "@/app/api/auth/logout/route";
import { POST as logoutAllRoute } from "@/app/api/auth/logout-all/route";
import { GET as meRoute } from "@/app/api/me/route";
import { PUT as followRoute } from "@/app/api/users/[handle]/follow/route";
import { makeUser } from "./factories";

const json = (body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) =>
  ({ method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...headers }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }) as RequestInit;
const none = undefined as never;

async function iosLogin(email = "ios@x.dev") {
  await signup(prisma, { email, password: "long enough pw", displayName: "I", handle: email.split("@")[0]!.replace(/\W/g, "_"), acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
  const res = await loginRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", json({ email, password: "long enough pw" }, { "x-client": "ios" })), none);
  return { res, body: (await res.json()) as { token: string; expiresAt: string; user: { handle: string } } };
}

describe("bearer tokens for iOS", () => {
  it("login with X-Client: ios returns { token, user } and sets no cookie; the session is stored hashed as IOS", async () => {
    const { res, body } = await iosLogin();
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.headers.get("set-cookie")).toBeNull();
    expect(body.token).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}$/);
    expect(body.user.handle).toBe("ios");
    const row = await prisma.session.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: hashToken(body.token) } });
    expect(row.client).toBe("IOS");
    expect(await prisma.session.findUnique({ where: { id: body.token } })).toBeNull();
    expect(row.expiresAt.getTime() - Date.now()).toBeGreaterThan(80 * 86_400_000);
  });

  it("signup with X-Client: ios also returns a token; web signup is unchanged (cookie, no token)", async () => {
    const ios = await signupRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/signup", json({ email: "s1@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "S", handle: "s_one", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" }, { "x-client": "ios" })), none);
    expect(ios.status).toBe(201);
    expect((await ios.json()).token).toBeTruthy();
    const web = await signupRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/signup", json({ email: "s2@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "S", handle: "s_two", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" })), none);
    expect(web.headers.get("set-cookie")).toMatch(/lc_session=/);
    expect((await web.json()).token).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("GET /api/me works with a bearer token and returns role and settings", async () => {
    const { body } = await iosLogin();
    const res = await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${body.token}` } }), none);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await res.json()).toMatchObject({ user: { handle: "ios", role: "USER" }, settings: { geofenceExempt: false } });
    expect((await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me"), none)).status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("a bad bearer token is unauthenticated even when a valid cookie is also sent", async () => {
    const { body } = await iosLogin();
    const res = await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: { authorization: "Bearer not-a-real-token-000000000", cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${body.token}` } }), none);
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });
});

describe("CSRF check applies to cookie auth only", () => {
  it("bearer requests from any Origin are accepted; the same cookie request from a foreign Origin is rejected", async () => {
    const target = await makeUser({ isPrivate: false });
    const { body } = await iosLogin();
    const ctx = { params: Promise.resolve({ handle: target.handle }) };
    const url = `http://localhost/api/users/${target.handle}/follow`;
    const put = (headers: Record<string, string>) => new Request(url, { method: "PUT", headers });
    const viaBearer = await followRoute(put({ authorization: `Bearer ${body.token}`, origin: "https://evil.example", host: "localhost" }), ctx);
    expect(viaBearer.status).toBe(200);
    const viaCookie = await followRoute(put({ cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${body.token}`, origin: "https://evil.example", host: "localhost" }), ctx);
    expect(viaCookie.status).toBe(403);
  });
});

describe("logout", () => {
  it("revokes only the current session; logout-all revokes every session", async () => {
    const { body: a } = await iosLogin();
    const second = await loginRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", json({ email: "ios@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" }, { "x-client": "ios" })), none);
    const b = (await second.json()) as { token: string };
    const auth = (t: string) => ({ authorization: `Bearer ${t}` });

    expect((await logoutRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST", headers: auth(a.token) }), none)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: auth(a.token) }), none)).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: auth(b.token) }), none)).status).toBe(200);

    const third = (await (await loginRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", json({ email: "ios@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" })), none)).json()) as object;
    expect(third).not.toHaveProperty("token");
    const before = await prisma.session.count(); // signup's session + b + the web login
    expect(before).toBe(3);
    const res = await logoutAllRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/logout-all", { method: "POST", headers: auth(b.token) }), none);
    expect(await res.json()).toMatchObject({ ok: true, revoked: before });
    expect((await meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers: auth(b.token) }), none)).status).toBe(401);
    expect(await prisma.session.count()).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe("token hardening", () => {
  const me = (headers: Record<string, string>) => meRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/me", { headers }), none);

  it("an expired bearer token gets 401 invalid_token with WWW-Authenticate, and the row is deleted", async () => {
    const u = await prisma.user.create({ data: { email: "e@x.dev", handle: "eee", displayName: "e", passwordHash: "x" } });
    const { createSession } = await import("@/lib/auth/session");
    const { token } = await createSession(prisma, u.id, { client: "IOS", now: new Date(Date.now() - 91 * 86_400_000) });
    const res = await me({ authorization: `Bearer ${token}` });
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(await res.json()).toMatchObject({ error: "invalid_token" });
    expect(res.headers.get("www-authenticate")).toMatch(/invalid_token/);
    expect(await prisma.session.count({ where: { userId: u.id } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it("a dead bearer token is rejected on public routes too (no silent anonymous fallback)", async () => {
    const { GET: places } = await import("@/app/api/places/route");
    expect((await places(new Request("http://localhost/api/places"), none)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await places(new Request("http://localhost/api/places", { headers: { authorization: "Bearer revoked-token-revoked-token-000" } }), none)).status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("accepts the auth scheme case-insensitively", async () => {
    const { body } = await iosLogin();
    expect((await me({ authorization: `bearer ${body.token}` })).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await me({ authorization: `BEARER   ${body.token}` })).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("binds tokens to their channel: iOS tokens aren't cookies and web tokens aren't bearer tokens", async () => {
    const { body } = await iosLogin();
    expect((await me({ cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${body.token}` })).status).toBe(401);
    const web = await loginRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", json({ email: "ios@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" })), none);
    const cookieToken = /lc_session=([^;]+)/.exec(web.headers.get("set-cookie") ?? "")![1]!;
    const row = await prisma.session.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: hashToken(cookieToken) } });
    expect(row.client).toBe("WEB");
    const ttlDays = (row.expiresAt.getTime() - Date.now()) / 86_400_000;
    expect(ttlDays).toBeGreaterThan(29);
    expect(ttlDays).toBeLessThanOrEqual(30);
    expect((await me({ authorization: `Bearer ${cookieToken}` })).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await me({ cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${cookieToken}` })).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("X-Client matching tolerates case and whitespace", async () => {
    await signup(prisma, { email: "w@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "W", handle: "www", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    const res = await loginRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", json({ email: "w@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" }, { "x-client": " IOS " })), none);
    expect((await res.json()).token).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("an invalid bearer with a foreign Origin gets 401, not a CSRF pass", async () => {
    const target = await makeUser({ isPrivate: false });
    const res = await followRoute(
      new Request(`http://localhost/api/users/${target.handle}/follow`, { method: "PUT", headers: { authorization: "Bearer forged-token-forged-token-000", origin: "https://evil.example", host: "localhost" } }),
      { params: Promise.resolve({ handle: target.handle }) },
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(await prisma.follow.count()).toBe(0);
  });

  it("logout: 401 for a dead bearer; cookie logout revokes the cookie session", async () => {
    const dead = await logoutRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST", headers: { authorization: "Bearer dead-token-dead-token-dead-00" } }), none);
    expect(dead.status).toBe(401);
    await signup(prisma, { email: "c@x.dev", password: "long enough pw", displayName: "C", handle: "ccc", acceptTerms: true, birthdate: "1990-05-05" });
    const web = await loginRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/login", json({ email: "c@x.dev", password: "long enough pw" })), none);
    const cookieToken = /lc_session=([^;]+)/.exec(web.headers.get("set-cookie") ?? "")![1]!;
    const out = await logoutRoute(new Request("http://localhost/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST", headers: { cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${cookieToken}` } }), none);
    expect(out.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await prisma.session.findUnique({ where: { id: hashToken(cookieToken) } })).toBeNull();
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/unit/age.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ageOn, isOldEnough, parseBirthdate } from "@/lib/age";

// 2026-10-02 12:00 in New York is 16:00Z.
const NOW = new Date("2026-10-02T16:00:00Z");

describe("parseBirthdate", () => {
  it("accepts real dates only", () => {
    expect(parseBirthdate("1990-05-05")).toEqual({ y: 1990, m: 5, d: 5 });
    expect(parseBirthdate("2000-02-29")).toEqual({ y: 2000, m: 2, d: 29 });
    for (const bad of ["2001-02-29", "2001-13-01", "2001-00-10", "2001-04-31", "05/05/1990", "1990-5-5", "", "yesterday", "1990-05-05T00:00:00Z"]) {
      expect(parseBirthdate(bad), bad).toBeNull();
    }
  });
});

describe("ageOn", () => {
  it("counts a birthday from its own date", () => {
    const birth = { y: 2005, m: 10, d: 2 };
    expect(ageOn(birth, { y: 2026, m: 10, d: 1 })).toBe(20);
    expect(ageOn(birth, { y: 2026, m: 10, d: 2 })).toBe(21);
    expect(ageOn(birth, { y: 2026, m: 10, d: 3 })).toBe(21);
  });
});

describe("isOldEnough (21+, New York today)", () => {
  it("is true on the 21st birthday and false the day before", () => {
    expect(isOldEnough("2005-10-02", NOW)).toBe(true);
    expect(isOldEnough("2005-10-03", NOW)).toBe(false);
    expect(isOldEnough("2005-10-01", NOW)).toBe(true);
  });
  it("handles leap-day birthdays", () => {
    expect(isOldEnough("2004-02-29", new Date("2025-03-01T17:00:00Z"))).toBe(true);
    expect(isOldEnough("2008-02-29", new Date("2029-02-28T17:00:00Z"))).toBe(false);
  });
  it("uses the New York date, not UTC: 11pm in New York is already the next day in UTC", () => {
    // 2026-10-02 23:30 in New York (EDT) is 2026-10-03 03:30Z. The 21st birthday on the 3rd is not here yet.
    expect(isOldEnough("2005-10-03", new Date("2026-10-03T03:30:00Z"))).toBe(false);
    expect(isOldEnough("2005-10-02", new Date("2026-10-03T03:30:00Z"))).toBe(true);
  });
  it("refuses future dates, nonsense and absurd ages", () => {
    expect(isOldEnough("2030-01-01", NOW)).toBe(false);
    expect(isOldEnough("1800-01-01", NOW)).toBe(false);
    expect(isOldEnough("nope", NOW)).toBe(false);
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/unit/content-filter.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { containsObjectionable } from "@/lib/content-filter";
import { assertCleanText } from "@/server/errors";

describe("objectionable content filter", () => {
  it("catches slurs and explicit terms", () => {
    for (const t of ["what a faggot", "total retard", "Kike", "nice pussy", "porn night", "coons", "whores"]) expect(containsObjectionable(t), t).toBe(true);
  });
  it("sees through leetspeak, accents, spacing, punctuation and letter stretching", () => {
    for (const t of ["n1gg3r", "f@gg0t", "r3t@rd", "f a g", "f.a.g.g.o.t", "faaaggggot", "what a w h o r e", "s l u t", "ćunt"]) expect(containsObjectionable(t), t).toBe(true);
  });
  it("catches unambiguous slurs inside run-together handles", () => {
    for (const t of ["xxniggerxx", "the_faggot_king", "Wetback99", "ilovecumshots", "big_n1gga_energy"]) expect(containsObjectionable(t), t).toBe(true);
  });
  it("does not flag innocent words that contain a short term (Scunthorpe problem)", () => {
    for (const t of [
      "Great cocktails tonight", "spicy margaritas", "a raccoon by the bins", "Scunthorpe United fans",
      "fire retardant curtains", "cocktail hour", "cumin in the fries", "Dickens themed trivia", "the grape soda", "Therapist night out",
      "Shitake mushrooms", "this place is fucking packed", "Pakistani food nearby", "cocoon lounge",
      // regressions from review: letter-collapsing and cross-word substrings used to flag these
      "pros and cons", "con artist", "Nigerian food spot", "zucchini gazpacho", "mini garden bar", "drag heads welcome",
      "wet backyard patio", "Cock & Bull", "cum laude", "someone tried to rape my friend here, be careful",
    ]) expect(containsObjectionable(t), t).toBe(false);
  });
  it("handles empty input", () => {
    expect(containsObjectionable("")).toBe(false);
    expect(containsObjectionable(null)).toBe(false);
  });
  it("assertCleanText throws a clear 400 naming the field", () => {
    expect(() => assertCleanText({ caption: "fine", comment: "you retard" })).toThrow(/Your comment contains language we don't allow/);
    try {
      assertCleanText({ handle: "sl_ut" });
    } catch (e) {
      expect(e).toMatchObject({ status: 400, code: "objectionable_content" });
    }
    expect(() => assertCleanText({ comment: "Packed, 20 min line" })).not.toThrow();
  });
});


====================================================================================================
== tests/unit/contract-requests.test.ts
====================================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { ZodTypeAny } from "zod";
import * as C from "@/contracts/api";
import { signupSchema } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { flagSchema, moderateSchema } from "@/server/services/moderation";
import { deleteAccountSchema } from "@/server/services/account";
import { stampEditSchema } from "@/server/services/stamps";

/** The app validates with the contract's request schemas; they must accept and reject exactly what the server does. */
function agree(contract: ZodTypeAny, server: ZodTypeAny, samples: unknown[]) {
  for (const s of samples) expect(contract.safeParse(s).success, JSON.stringify(s)).toBe(server.safeParse(s).success);
}

describe("contract request schemas agree with server validation", () => {
  it("signup", () =>
    agree(C.SignupRequest, signupSchema, [
